Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves language-model reasoning, but how these gains relate to inference-time decoding and search remains unclear. Does RL create reasoning the base model lacks, or shift the rollout distribution toward trajectories it can already reach but rarely samples? We study this behaviorally with a Unified Decoding Framework (UDF), which expresses token-level sampling, beam-like search, tree search, and sequence-level resampling as executable policies over a shared budgeted operating space, scored post hoc with pass@$k$, self-consistency, best-of-$N$, and first-finish success. Using paired Base/RL checkpoints from SimpleRL-Zoo, we ask whether an RL default-policy curve can be approximated by a structured path of Base operating points. On Math500, AIME, GPQA, and IFEval, the pass@$k$ recovery path follows a Budgeted Operating-Point Transition Rule (BOPTR), $N_{\mathrm{Base}} \approx αN_{\mathrm{RL}}^β$, with benchmark-conditioned exponents. On Qwen2.5-7B, BOPTR gives the lowest transfer error among the non-oracle rules we test, 3.41 pp (95% CI [2.32, 5.53]); a three-seed replication gives 3.07 $\pm$ 0.39 pp. The rule extends to ten models across four families (3.28 to 4.87 pp on checkpoints added after fitting), to four benchmarks it was never fitted on (5.03 pp vs. 4.44 pp in fit), and holds without an RL checkpoint for the target model (4.19 pp) or without RL supervision of any kind (5.08 pp). These results support a qualified internalized-search reading: under the recipe we test, much of the measured RL gain corresponds to a change in sampling efficiency toward operating points the base model can already reach under search. We treat the scaling patterns as descriptive of this recipe and cohort, report where they break down, and use UDF and BOPTR as behavioral diagnostics rather than evidence of parameter-level equivalence.

</details>


### [218] [Some Emotions Run Deeper: Layer-wise Probing and Causal Intervention in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01279)
*Tian Fang,Gaël Guibon,Davide Buscaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）中情感信息的可访问深度并非固定属性，而是显著依赖于文本来源的显式程度和语境化水平。通过跨数据集、多模型的层间探测与干预分析，发现情感表征从浅层到深层系统分布，且特定层级对情感分类至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多使用单一语料库进行LLM情感分析的层间探测，未能确定情感可访问的深度是模型固有属性还是受文本源特性影响。本研究旨在探究不同显式度和语境化的文本源如何影响LLM内部情感表征的分布位置。

Method: 选取Twitter帖子、Reddit评论和自传体叙事三个不同显式度的数据集，以及8个1B-9B参数的开源LLM（Llama, Qwen, Granite系列）。结合层间探测、离线特征缩放、在线前向干预、迁移分析和早期退出分类器进行综合评估。

Result: (1) 最佳探测层随语料库变化：从接近输入的浅层到超过模型深度一半的深层，该顺序在匹配标签长度分布后依然保持；(2) 针对探测选定层带的正向传播干预导致测试准确率下降5-6个点，显著优于随机层带；(3) 选定层带在不同数据集和情感类别间具有迁移性，表明存在部分共享的情感信息而非严格独立的子空间；(4) 基于探测选定的早期退出表示平均比全深度退出表现高出6.9个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM中情感信息的内部表征深度高度依赖于输入文本的性质。模型并非在所有层级均匀编码情感，而是根据文本的显式程度在特定层带集中存储情感信息。利用这些特定的层带进行高效推理（如早期退出）能显著提升性能并降低计算成本。

Abstract: Emotion is expressed in text along a wide spectrum, from surface lexical cues to inferences entangled with content. Most layer-wise analyses of emotion in LLMs use a single corpus, leaving open whether the depth at which emotion becomes accessible is a property of the model or also of the text source. We investigate this across three datasets spanning different degrees of explicitness and contextualization in emotion expression (Twitter posts, Reddit comments, and autobiographical narratives) and eight 1B--9B open-weight LLMs from the Llama, Qwen, and Granite families. We combine layer-wise probing with offline feature scaling and online forward interventions, transfer analyses, and an early-exit classifier. We find that (i) the best probing layer shifts systematically across corpora, from input-adjacent layers to over half model depth, and this ordering persists after matching label-by-length-bin distributions; (ii) across the evaluated settings, forward-pass interventions on probe-selected bands reduce test accuracy by 5--6 points more than same-width random bands ($q < 0.01$); (iii) selected bands transfer across datasets and emotion categories, suggesting partially shared affective information rather than strictly per-emotion substrates; and (iv) probe-selected early-exit representations outperform full-depth exits by $6.9$ percentage points on average.

</details>


### [219] [Explore Before Committing: Hypothesis-Guided Search for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01294)
*Ruochen Zhou,Zhengyu Chen,Luan Zhang,Siyang Gao,Yee Whye Teh,Shiqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HypoSearch方法，通过生成轻量级假设并并行探索多个分支，在承诺最终答案前比较证据，解决深度研究代理因过早锁定单一搜索路径导致的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究代理常因在早期状态未收集足够对比证据就沿单一方向搜索而陷入错误路径，导致后续工具调用强化该误导性方向，增加失败风险。

Method: 提出HypoSearch：生成轻量级假设作为软搜索提示，通过有界的独立分支进行探索，并在承诺前比较各分支层面的证据；同时利用行为信号筛选训练数据。

Result: 在四个深度研究基准和三个骨干模型上，HypoSearch优于单轨迹搜索及标准并行基线；使Qwen3.5-122B在BC-small上得分从46.7提升至60.0，且工具调用次数少于五个独立轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过并行探索与证据比较机制，HypoSearch有效降低了搜索偏差，提升了复杂问答的准确性与效率，且其捕捉的行为模式有助于优化训练数据质量。

Abstract: Deep-research agents answer complex questions by interacting with search and browsing tools, yet they often search along a single evolving trajectory. Our trajectory-level analysis reveals a common failure mode in which the agent may encounter an early search state with several plausible directions, but follow one direction before collecting enough comparative evidence. Once this happens, subsequent tool calls tend to reinforce the same path, increasing the chance of failure when the initial direction is misleading. We further find that successful trajectories reduce this risk through two behaviors: grounding vague exploration in concrete candidates and shifting directions when the current path is weak or incomplete. Based on these findings, we propose HypoSearch, which generates lightweight hypotheses as soft search hints, explores them through bounded independent branches, and compares branch-level evidence before commitment. Across four deep-research benchmarks and three backbone models, HypoSearch consistently outperforms single-trajectory search and standard parallel baselines, improving Qwen3.5-122B from 46.7 to 60.0 on BC-small while using fewer tool calls than five independent trajectories. A pilot supervised fine-tuning study further shows that these behavioral signals can curate compact training trajectories and reduce degradation from unfiltered data.

</details>


### [220] [Exploring Sparse Autoencoders in Text-Based Causal Confounding Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01322)
*Mian Zhong,Katherine A. Keith,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的因果调整流水线，通过迭代选择最小特征集来解决文本表示中偏差与方差的权衡问题，在模拟评估中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在处理文本数据的因果问题时，构建文本表示存在权衡：既要足够大/密集以保留无偏估计所需的混杂变量信息，又要足够小/稀疏以满足有限样本重叠并降低方差。现有方法难以同时优化这两点。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs），提出一种新的因果调整流水线，通过条件独立性测试迭代选择最小的SAE特征子集。使用半合成数据（包括二元和多标签混杂变量）进行评估。

Result: SAE表示在标准半合成评估中实现了比替代表示更好的调整效果（更低的偏差和更高的覆盖率）。其可解释性提供了验证机会。对于更复杂的多标签混杂设置，现成的调整方法需要进一步研究。

Conclusion: 基于SAE的因果调整方法能有效平衡偏差与方差，提供更准确的因果效应估计，并为复杂文本混杂场景下的因果推断提供了新途径。

Abstract: In many settings, studying causal questions based on text data requires adjusting for confounding information within texts. Yet there is a tradeoff in constructing text representations for adjustment: they must be sufficiently large and/or dense to preserve the confounding variables necessary for unbiased effect estimation, but sufficiently small and/or sparse to satisfy finite-sample overlap and yield low-variance estimates. To address this tradeoff, we turn to sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and propose a novel causal adjustment pipeline that iteratively selects a minimal set of SAE features via conditional independence tests. We find that SAE representations achieve better adjustments (lower bias and and higher coverage) than alternative representations in standard semi-synthetic evaluations with binary confounders, and their interpretability offers opportunities for falsification. We also introduce a more realistic semi-synthetic evaluation that uses multi-label data as the unobserved confounders and find off-the-shelf adjustment methods require increased investigation for these more complex settings. Code: https://github.com/mianzg/sae-text-confounder

</details>


### [221] [Probing Factual Knowledge Transfer with Training Data Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01341)
*Romina Oji,Marc Braun,Marcel Bollmann,Marco Kuhlmann,Jenny Kunz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建SIFT资源并采用干预式框架，证明多语言模型在持续预训练期间，跨语言的事实知识迁移非常有限；模型主要依赖目标语言数据，且浅层关联启发式方法会夸大迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更可靠地回答多语言语言模型在持续预训练期间是否会将事实知识跨语言迁移，还是主要回忆直接从目标语言数据中学习到的事实这一问题。

Method: 提出基于干预的框架：从英语预训练模型开始，对波斯语数据进行持续预训练，并从不同粒度系统性地移除特定事实。构建SIFT资源（500个三元组，20种关系），按文化起源分层，并使用原生波斯语完形填空模板进行评估。

Result: 1. 事实迁移非常有限，在严格移除条件下，大多数英语获取的事实无法迁移到波斯语。2. 句子级共现移除不足以消除事实信号。3. 较易的负样本集通过奖励浅层关联启发式方法夸大了表观迁移效果，而较难的候选集表现显著更低。4. 源语言实体频率影响巨大，波斯相关事实因在英语语料中罕见，几乎不迁移。

Conclusion: 多语言模型中的跨语言事实知识迁移能力受限，模型更多依赖于目标语言特定的数据而非深层的跨语言知识共享，评估时需警惕浅层启发式方法带来的偏差。

Abstract: Do multilingual language models transfer factual knowledge across languages during continued pretraining, or do they mostly recall facts learned directly from the target-language data? To answer this question more reliably, we propose an intervention-based framework: starting from an English-pretrained model, we continue pretraining on Persian data from which specific facts have been systematically removed at varying levels of granularity. We construct SIFT, a resource of 500 triples across 20 relations, stratified by the cultural origin of each fact's subject into general (globally prominent) and Persian-related entities, designed for both systematic fact removal from training data and evaluation, with natively written Persian cloze templates. Our results show that fact transfer is very limited: under the strictest removal condition, a large majority of English-acquired facts fail to transfer into Persian. We further show that sentence-level co-occurrence removal is insufficient to eliminate fact signal, and that easier (randomly selected) negative candidate sets substantially inflate apparent transfer by rewarding shallow associative heuristics, while performance on a harder candidate set that allows for less reliance on heuristics is much lower. Finally, we show that source-language entity frequency has a large influence, with Persian-related facts, which are orders of magnitude rarer in the English corpus, hardly transferring.

</details>


### [222] [CHARM: Character Hallucination for Multicultural Role Play Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01352)
*Sunkyung Han,Nahyeon Park,Gaeun Seo,Seunghyun Yoon,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CHARM基准，揭示大语言模型角色扮演中的幻觉主要源于“边界合规失败”（即明知越界仍回答），而非单纯的“边界意识缺失”，且存在文化偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估难以区分角色幻觉是源于未能识别知识边界，还是识别了边界但未遵守。需要一种能分离这两种错误的评估方法，并涵盖多元文化背景。

Method: 1. 构建CHARM基准：包含来自五个文化-语言区域的40个真实/虚构角色，由母语者验证。2. 设计两种边界类型：时间边界（历史vs现代）和跨宇宙边界（叙事/历史范围外实体）。3. 采用两阶段评估：分别测试“边界意识”（是否承认问题超纲）和“边界合规”（是否在具体问题上拒绝回答/弃权）。4. 使用支持弃权的选择题进行探测。

Result: 1. 幻觉主要由合规失败驱动：模型常承认问题超出角色知识范围，但仍提供事实性、不符合角色的答案。2. 参数覆盖现象：确认大量案例中模型存储了相关事实但未能抑制。3. 文化差异：不同文化区域角色的失败率存在系统性差异，反映训练数据中的表征不平衡。

Conclusion: 角色扮演LLM的核心问题在于无法在拥有外部知识时抑制其输出以符合角色设定，且这种能力受文化数据分布影响。需改进模型对角色边界的遵循机制。

Abstract: Role-playing large language models (LLMs) are expected to adopt a character's style while also respecting that character's knowledge boundaries. Prior evaluations detect character hallucination but rarely distinguish whether errors arise from failure to recognize a boundary or from failure to comply despite recognition. We introduce CHARM, a multicultural benchmark of 40 real and fictional characters drawn from five cultural-linguistic regions, and validated by native reviewers. It probes two boundary types, Temporal (historical vs. modern) and Cross-Universe (entities outside a character's narrative or historical universe), using abstention-enabled multiple-choice questions. We propose a two-stage evaluation that separates Boundary-Awareness (explicit recognition that a query is out of scope) from Boundary-Compliance (abstention when answering concrete questions). Evaluations across six LLMs show that hallucination is driven predominantly by compliance failures. Models frequently acknowledge that a query lies outside the character's knowledge yet still provide factual, out-of-character answers. By re-posing the same questions to the target character, we confirm that a large fraction of these cases are verified parametric overrides; the model stores the relevant fact but fails to suppress it. We also observe systematic cultural variation in these failures, consistent with imbalances in how characters from different regions are represented in model knowledge.

</details>


### [223] [Where the Verifier Fails: A Category-Level Audit of Reward Signals in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01354)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过形态测试分解了自动验证器在数学答案验证中的错误来源，发现主要问题集中在空白和标点符号（尤其是尾随句号和换行符）的处理上，而非复杂的LaTeX解析；同时揭示了不同配置间巨大的自验证差异及数值容差的尺度不变性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作指出评估工具仅接受约94%的地面真实答案，并将此归咎于LaTeX解析问题，但该聚合数据未能说明具体是哪些答案形式导致了错误预算的消耗，因此需要更细致的误差分解以理解验证器的实际失效机制。

Method: 对验证器而非模型应用形态测试，生成保持数学意义的认证等效答案变体（重写），从而识别出可证明的假阴性；在307,420个裁决中，针对四个广泛使用的验证器按答案类别测量拒绝率。

Result: 1. 自验证范围从53.8%到95.2%，同一库的不同配置在49.9%的对数上存在分歧；2. 误差主要集中在空白和标点符号（占默认LaTeX配置下违约失败的93.0%），尾随句号和换行符是主要因素；3. 区分拒绝与执行失败后发现，相似总错误的验证器失效原因相反，且参考数值级联对“差一”错误的接受率随数量级呈阶跃变化。

Conclusion: 自动验证器的性能瓶颈并非主要来自复杂的语法解析，而是简单的格式处理（如空格和标点）；此外，验证器的行为具有高度的实现依赖性和尺度敏感性，需关注具体的配置细节和数值容差策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and standard benchmark evaluation both rely on an automatic verifier that turns a free text answer into a binary reward. Prior work reports that one evaluation harness accepts only about 94% of its own ground truth answers, blaming LaTeX parsing. That is an aggregate: it does not say which answer forms consume the error budget. We supply the decomposition. We apply metamorphic testing to the verifier rather than the model, generating certified equivalent answer variants, that is, rewrites that preserve mathematical meaning by construction, so that any rejection is a provable false negative needing no human adjudication. We then measure rejection per answer category across four widely used verifiers over 307,420 verdicts. We find three things. (1) Self validation ranges from 53.8% to 95.2% on identical inputs, a spread of 41.3 points. The published figure describes one implementation, not the task; two configurations of the same library disagree on 49.9% of pairs. (2) The residual is not spread across parsing categories but concentrated in whitespace and punctuation, which account for 93.0% of in contract failures for the default LaTeX configuration. A trailing period or newline dominates the budget. (3) Separating rejection from execution failure shows that verifiers with similar aggregate error fail for opposite reasons, and that a reference numeric cascade accepts off by one wrong answers as a step function of magnitude, from 0% below 10^4 to 100% at or above, because its relative tolerance is scale invariant.

</details>


### [224] [Separating Syntax from Language: A Mechanistic Account of Translation in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01356)
*Mikhail Sonkin,Tanja Baeumel,Daniil Gurgurov,Josef van Genabith,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了多语言大模型（mLLMs）在翻译过程中，输出语言的生产阶段可进一步解耦为独立的句法和表层语言过程。通过因果干预和探测分析，证实模型先构建目标词语序，再实现表层形式，并识别出对句法变换敏感但独立于语言身份的注意力头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言大模型在机器翻译中表现优异，但其将一种语言的表示转换为另一种语言的具体机制尚不明确。 prior work 认为翻译包含概念内容表示和特定语言形式生产两个分离过程，本研究旨在进一步探索这一过程的模块化程度及内部结构。

Method: 构建隔离跨语言词序差异的控制性多语言数据集；采用因果干预（causal interventions）和探测技术（probing），追踪翻译过程中表示的转换路径；分析注意力头对句法变换和语言身份的敏感性。

Result: 1. 翻译的输出语言生产阶段可分离为句法过程和表层语言过程；2. 模型首先构建目标侧的词序，随后才实现目标语言的表层形式；3. 识别出个别注意力头，它们对句法变换选择性敏感，但对语言身份基本不变。

Conclusion: 确立了句法结构承诺作为翻译中的独立阶段，扩展了之前的分解理论，展示了 mLLMs 如何通过功能不同的组件实现翻译，表明翻译比先前假设的更具模块化特征。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (mLLMs) achieve strong performance in machine translation, yet our understanding of the mechanisms by which they transform representations from one language to another remains incomplete. Prior work suggests that translation decomposes into separable processes within an mLLM, where conceptual content is first represented independently, followed by a production into language-specific form. In this work, we show that translation is even more modular than previously assumed and that the output language production in translation processes is actually further separable into a syntax and a surface language process. We construct controlled multilingual datasets that isolate cross-linguistic differences in word-order and use causal interventions and probing to track how representations are transformed during translation. We find that models first construct target-side word-order before realizing the target language surface form. We identify individual attention heads that are selectively sensitive to syntactic transformations while remaining largely invariant to language identity. These results establish the commitment to a syntactic structure as an independent stage in translation, extending prior decompositions and showing how translation is implemented by functionally different components within mLLMs.

</details>


### [225] [Investigating Linear Probe Robustness to Linguistic Register, Medical Specialty, and Corpus Shifts in Medical QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01361)
*Nishant Mishra,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过构建医疗QA基准，隔离写作风格、领域和语料库变量，发现大语言模型（LLM）中的“真理方向”对写作风格和医学专科具有鲁棒性，但在不同语料库间迁移时性能下降，表明线性探针恢复的信号部分绑定于数据集结构而非纯粹的医学知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究关于线性探针能否泛化到输入分布变化存在争议，且由于交叉数据集实验混淆了多种输入变化因素，导致结论难以解释。本研究旨在隔离并单独评估写作风格、领域和语料库对真理方向稳定性的影响。

Method: 构建了包含500个MedQA条目的基准，每个条目改写为四种风格（教科书、患者、临床笔记、口语），标注临床专科，并与MedMCQA和MMLU-medical结合用于跨数据集评估。对四个开源LLM（2-8B参数）进行探测分析。

Result: 真理方向对写作风格（AUROC变化约0.10）和医学专科（AUROC变化约0.03）表现出高度鲁棒性；但在语料库迁移中性能不均等下降，在MMLU-medical上下降0.12 AUROC，在MedMCQA上下降0.21 AUROC。结果在第二生成器和人工撰写的患者问题中得到复现。

Conclusion: 真理方向在医学领域内基本稳定，但在某些语料库转换下失效。问题格式并非导致失效的原因，这表明线性探针恢复的信号部分受限于数据集结构，而不仅仅是医学知识本身。

Abstract: Linear classifiers trained on hidden states of a large language model (LLM), linear probes, can flag factual errors from a single forward pass. Geometrically, that implies that true and false statements separate along a stable direction in hidden state space, i.e., the truth direction. Prior work disagrees on whether this generalises across input shifts, but the disagreement is hard to interpret because cross-dataset probe transfer experiments confound several kinds of input change at once. We isolate three such variables in medical question-answering (QA): writing style (register), domain (medical specialty), and corpus (dataset). We build a benchmark using 500 MedQA entries, each rewritten into four styles (textbook, patient, clinical note, colloquial), annotated with clinical specialty, and grouped with two other exam corpora, MedMCQA and MMLU-medical, for cross-dataset evaluation. Probing four open-weight LLMs (2--8B), we find that the truth direction is largely robust to writing style (mean $Δ_\text{register} \approx 0.10$ AUROC on held-out facts) and to medical specialty ($Δ_\text{specialty} \approx 0.03$), but degrades unevenly across corpora: by $0.12$ AUROC on MMLU-medical and by $0.21$ on MedMCQA, roughly twice the register gap. The register result replicates with a second generator and carries over to human-written patient questions. The truth direction is therefore largely stable within the medical domain but breaks under some corpus shifts, and question format does not explain the break, which suggests that the signal a linear probe recovers is partly bound to dataset structure rather than to medical knowledge alone.

</details>


### [226] [How Correct Is Your Answer? A Semantic Correctness Framework for Open QA Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01369)
*Elitsa Yotkova,Violeta Kastreva,Petar Velkov,Hristo Boyanov,Dimitar Dimitrov,Ivan Koychev,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决大模型开放问答评估难题，提出语义正确性分类体系、CAP-Correctness基准及基于双向NLI的CAP指标，显著提升评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放问答评估难以区分答案的表面形式差异及定性错误（如不完整、矛盾、幻觉），传统度量标准无法有效捕捉这些细微差别。

Method: 构建包含八个有序类别的语义正确性分类法；发布8.8k样本的CAP-Correctness基准和11k样本的CAP-Statements数据集；提出参考基础的CAP（上下文感知精度）指标，利用双向自然语言推理对问题进行条件评分。

Result: 在单调性协议测试中，CAP指标优于现有的基线方法，能更准确地反映答案的正确性排序。

Conclusion: 提出的分类体系、数据集和CAP指标为现代大模型的开放问答评估提供了更可靠、细粒度的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended question answering remains a bottleneck for measuring answer correctness of modern LLMs. Unlike multiple-choice tasks, free-form answers may be correct in many surface forms and may fail in qualitatively different ways, including incompleteness, contradiction, overgeneration, and endorsement of false premises. Existing judgment-based and similarity-based metrics often collapse these distinctions. We address this gap with three reusable contributions. First, we introduce a semantic correctness taxonomy that assigns open-ended answers to eight ordered classes, separating verbose-but-correct answers from those contaminated by hallucinated content. Second, we release CAP-Correctness, an 8.8k-example benchmark spanning widely used QA datasets, and CAP-Statements, an 11k-example dataset for converting question-answer pairs into declarative statements for natural language inference (NLI) training and statement-based evaluation. Third, we introduce CAP (Context-Aware Precision), a reference-based metric that scores question-conditioned statements using bidirectional NLI. Under a monotonicity protocol testing whether metrics respect the taxonomy's intended ordering, CAP outperforms established baselines.

</details>


### [227] [Behaviorally Effective LoRA Writes Are Sparse and Structured](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01374)
*Haruto Sato,Yuki Tanaka,Ren Nakamura,Aoi Kobayashi,Mei Ito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示LoRA更新中行为有效的部分具有稀疏性和结构性，并通过Learned-Basis LoRA方法证实了“写入几何结构”是因果状态变量，关键参数组件（如q_proj, o_proj, down_proj）在少量维度上产生主要行为影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适应（LoRA）固定了更新的秩，但未能识别训练好的模型中哪些具体部分承载了行为变化。本文旨在直接研究这一问题，探究行为有效LoRA写入的结构特征。

Method: 提出Learned-Basis LoRA，这是一种学习基延续配方：先预热无约束适配器，将其学到的写入列转换为模块级正交基并冻结该基，然后在受限参数化内继续训练。通过14次从无约束到受限形式的精确切换验证准确性保持不变，并利用无重投影测试和单方向消融实验分析写入空间的有效性。

Result: 行为有效的LoRA写入是稀疏、结构化且高度集中的。在GSM8K、MathQA和AQuA数据集上，每模块前k个维度的延续在k={2, 4}时达到最优；全局排名测试显示学到的top-16/top-32子集优于随机子集。单方向消融表明，少数晚期的q_proj、o_proj和down_proj组件具有巨大的行为影响力。

Conclusion: LoRA的有用信号集中在特定的学习写入空间内，而非随机分布或PCA控制空间中。“写入几何结构”被确立为因果状态变量，证明了行为有效更新的高度稀疏性和结构性。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation fixes the rank of the update, but it does not identify which parts of a trained
  write actually carry behavior. We study that question directly and show that behaviorally effective
  LoRA writes are sparse, structured, and far more concentrated than the raw low-rank parameterization
  suggests.
  We use Learned-Basis LoRA, a learned-basis continuation recipe, to expose that structure. The recipe
  warms up an unconstrained adapter, converts its learned write columns into a module-wise orthonormal
  basis, freezes that basis, and continues training inside the constrained parameterization. Across 14
  exact switches from unconstrained to constrained form, held-out accuracy is unchanged at the
  conversion step and reconstructed write matrices differ by at most 0.25% relative Frobenius error.
  Same-state continuation then shows that the same trained checkpoint develops differently under
  different write subspaces, establishing write geometry as a causal state variable. A no-retraining
  projection test shows that useful write signal stays inside the learned write space and largely
  disappears from random or frozen-activation PCA controls.
  The concentration pattern is strong at both local and global scales. Across GSM8K, MathQA, and AQuA,
  per-module top-k continuation reaches its optimum at k in {2, 4} in all twelve seed-level cases we
  test. A stricter global ranking test shows that learned top-16 and top-32 subsets outperform matched
  random subsets, especially on GSM8K/Qwen and MathQA/Qwen. Single-direction ablations further reveal a
  sparse set of late q_proj, o_proj, and down_proj components with outsized behavioral impact.

</details>


### [228] [Polish ModernBERT: The Long and Short of Polish Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01379)
*Michał Perełkiewicz,Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Polish ModernBERT，一种基于ModernBERT架构的波兰语编码器家族（含Base/Large及512/8K上下文变体），在30个任务中超越现有波兰语模型，显著提升了长上下文性能与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Encoder-only Transformers在判别和表征学习任务中有效，但现有的波兰语编码器仍主要依赖传统的BERT/RoBERTa架构，缺乏针对长上下文和高效率优化的现代模型。

Method: 采用ModernBERT预训练配方，通过分阶段选择实验构建四个规模的波兰语编码器；发布涵盖法律分类、意识形态预测、事实一致性评估及人权违规评估的长上下文基准测试。

Result: 在30个任务中取得最佳整体性能（Base-8K得分83.99，Large-8K得分85.11）；长上下文任务中相比RoBERTa-8K基线显著提升（Base从67.47升至77.15）；Base-8K模型参数量减少22%且具备更低的峰值内存使用和延迟；在3亿参数以下模型中检索基准表现最佳。

Conclusion: Polish ModernBERT通过引入现代预训练策略，在保持高效率的同时大幅提升了波兰语模型在长上下文场景下的性能，为波兰语NLP任务提供了更优的编码器选择。

Abstract: Encoder-only Transformers remain effective for discriminative and representation-learning tasks, yet Polish encoders still largely rely on BERT/RoBERTa-style architectures. We introduce \textbf{Polish ModernBERT}, a family of four Polish encoders available at Base and Large scales, each with 512-token and 8K context variants. We adapt the ModernBERT pretraining recipe through staged selection experiments and release a long-context benchmark covering legal topic classification, ideological decision-direction prediction, factual-consistency assessment over literary plot summaries, and human-rights violation assessment. Across 30 tasks, Polish ModernBERT achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated Polish encoders, reaching 83.99 and 85.11 for the Base-8K and Large-8K models, respectively. On long-context tasks, the 8K variants improve over matched Polish RoBERTa-8K baselines from 67.47 to 77.15 and from 75.88 to 78.49 at the Base and Large scales, respectively. The Base-8K model achieves this gain with 22\% fewer parameters (149M vs.\ 190M). Efficiency measurements in representative inference setups show lower peak memory usage and latency than matched Polish RoBERTa baselines in both 512-token and 8K settings. Polish ModernBERT-8K-Base additionally achieves the best result on a Polish retrieval benchmark among the evaluated encoders below 300M parameters.

</details>


### [229] [InSight: A Benchmark for Agentic Claim Verification in Interactive Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01383)
*Maeve Hutchinson,Syed Mahbubul Huq,Mohammad Albinhassan,Radu Jianu,Aidan Slingsby,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出InSight基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在交互式可视化环境中的主动代理式声明验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多局限于静态图像和一次性问答，无法捕捉动态数据分析中证据被遮挡、分布或需通过用户交互才能揭示的认知需求。

Method: 构建包含21,349个源自人类分析叙述的声明数据集，嵌入完全交互式的Web环境中；要求智能体通过导航环境来判定自然语言声明是否得到支持、反驳或不可验证，并将交互轨迹作为推理代理进行审计。

Result: 评估了最先进的模型，发现交互式验证仍是一个非平凡的挑战，证明了现有模型在处理动态、交互式视觉证据时的局限性。

Conclusion: InSight填补了动态交互式可视化分析的评估空白，为研究模型如何搜索和综合视觉证据提供了严谨的测试框架，并公开了相关数据集。

Abstract: Vision Language Models have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in interpreting static visual artifacts, but modern data analysis is inherently dynamic, requiring the active interrogation of interactive environments. Existing benchmarks are predominantly constrained to static imagery and one-shot question answering and fail to capture the epistemic demands of this domain, where evidence is frequently occluded, distributed across linked views, or conditionally revealed through user agency. In this paper, we introduce InSight, a benchmark for agentic claim verification over interactive visualizations. The dataset consists of 21,349 claims derived from human-authored analytical narratives and grounded in fully interactive web-based environments. Agents must navigate these environments to determine whether a natural language claim is supported, refuted or not verifiable given the available evidence. Unlike traditional evaluations, InSight treats interaction traces as intrinsic proxies for reasoning, enabling a rigorous audit of how models seek and synthesize visual evidence. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, revealing that interactive verification remains a non-trivial challenge. We release InSight at https://github.com/maevehutch/insight.

</details>


### [230] [When Tokenization is Secretly Output Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01386)
*Tanja Baeumel,Josef van Genabith,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出语言模型中的分词不仅是输入预处理，更决定了输出监督信号。通过数值推理实验证明，输出分词策略显著影响模型性能、训练动态及内部表示，而输入分词影响较小。现有研究常忽略这一差异，导致模型比较存在偏差。提出“输出监督”框架来解释分词对模型行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有文献中缺乏对分词如何影响模型性能的机制性解释，并纠正大量对比研究中因忽视输出分词差异而导致的评估偏差。

Method: 1. 在数值推理任务中进行受控实验，解耦输入与输出分词；2. 分析不同分词粒度下的任务表现、训练动态和模型内部表示；3. 调查120篇近期关于数值推理的顶级会议论文，统计其对分词策略的报告情况。

Result: 1. 输出分词差异导致显著的模型性能和内部表示变化，且该效应相对于输入分词具有不变性；2. 120篇调查中仅10%报告了数值分词细节，69%在未报告的情况下进行了跨分词对比，证实了普遍存在的评估疏忽。

Conclusion: 将分词视为“输出监督”而非单纯的输入预处理，提供了理解分词影响模型行为的理论框架。未来的模型比较必须明确报告并控制分词策略，以区分能力差异与任务定义差异。

Abstract: Tokenization in language models is treated by default as an input preprocessing decision. We argue that this framing is incomplete: in autoregressive models, tokenizer granularity determines what the model must resolve in a single forward pass, and therefore the supervision signal it receives. This affects both the difficulty of the learning problem and the representations that emerge inside the model. We test this in a controlled experiment on numeric reasoning with a novel decoupling of input and output tokenization. As the output supervision view predicts, differences in task performance, training dynamics, and model internals are induced by output tokenization and largely invariant to input tokenization. This may matter in practice, because models with different tokenization strategies differ not only in input representation but in the task they were trained on. Comparisons between models may thus partly reflect task definition rather than ability. A survey of 120 recent *CL papers on numeric reasoning confirms that this is rarely acknowledged: only about 10% report the numeric tokenization of the models they evaluate, while 69% compare across tokenization, and thus supervision, regimes without reporting it. While prior work documents that tokenization consistently affects model performance, there is no principled account of why. We argue that framing tokenization as output supervision provides that account.

</details>


### [231] [From Rollouts to Recipes: Self-Contained Post-Training for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01422)
*Yifei Li,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Zihan Ma,Jiashuai Liu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Routing框架，根据模型自身rollout的正确性和置信度动态分配不同优化策略（GRPO、自蒸馏、正则化或跳过），无需外部标注即可提升大语言模型后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练方法对所有样本应用单一训练配方，忽略了模型rollout揭示的样本级学习状态差异，导致效率低下或更新冗余。

Method: 构建行为条件化的后训练框架Self-Routing，利用rollout正确性和置信度作为路由依据，将样本动态分配至GRPO、on-policy自蒸馏、正则化或跳过四种策略之一，实现自适应优化。

Result: 在Qwen3和Qwen3.5骨干网络上的数学推理实验中，Self-Routing性能持续优于均匀GRPO、均匀OPSD、固定混合及简单路由基线；分析显示路由分布随训练变化，减少了对低信号或已稳定样本的冗余更新。

Conclusion: 基于模型自身行为状态进行动态路由优化，能有效适应不同样本的学习需求，提升后训练效率与最终性能，且无需额外监督信号。

Abstract: Post-training large language models usually applies a single training recipe to all samples, even though the model's own rollouts reveal different sample-level learning states. We propose Self-Routing, a behavior-conditioned post-training framework that uses rollout correctness and confidence to decide how each sample should be optimized. Depending on its behavior state, a sample is routed to GRPO, on-policy self-distillation, regularization, or skipping, allowing training to adapt without external teachers, extra annotations, or additional sampling. Experiments on mathematical reasoning across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 backbones show that Self-Routing consistently improves over uniform GRPO, uniform OPSD, fixed mixtures, and simpler routing baselines. Further analyses show that the routing distribution changes over training and reduces unnecessary updates on low-signal or already stable samples.

</details>


### [232] [GlossoGen: Emergent Language in Complex Multi-Agent LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01491)
*Elias Stengel-Eskin,Newton Sander,Carlos Bonetti,Sasha Boguraev,James Bowler,Hale Sirin,Simon Kirby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GlossoGen平台及SaveVeyru场景，证实LLM智能体间能演化出具有组合性和形态生产力的新语言。这些语言因偏离英语先验而对人类不可读。研究揭示了效率压力、模型强度及“事后”阶段对语言演化的关键作用，并指出LLM具备类似人类的累积文化进化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）智能体间交互频率的增加，亟需探究多智能体环境下的语言演变机制，以评估其对安全性、可监控性及语言学理论的影响。

Method: 构建GlossoGen平台，开发包含部分信息和高压力沟通需求的SaveVeyru场景，观察不同条件下LLM智能体的语言交互与演变过程。

Result: 1. LLM智能体间确实发生了语言演变，生成的语言具有组合性和形态生产力，但因偏离英语先验而对人类不可理解。2. 关键驱动因素包括效率压力、底层模型强度及允许约定惯例的“事后”阶段。3. 新语言由强模型生成，弱模型可通过使用习得已有语言；智能体通过主动学习而非被动接收掌握新语言。

Conclusion: 当前LLM展现出此前仅在人类中观察到的累积文化进化潜力，混合智能体群体能够发展出超越其最低共同标准的语言能力。

Abstract: The growing rate at which LLM agents interact with one another raises key questions about language evolution in multi-LLM-agent settings, with implications for safety and monitorability as well as for linguistic accounts of LLMs. To address these questions, we introduce GlossoGen, a novel platform for studying multi-agent language evolution in complex scenarios. Within GlossoGen, we build the SaveVeyru scenario, which requires agents with partial information to communicate under pressure. We find that language evolution does occur between LLM agents, that the resulting languages are compositional and morphologically productive, and that they deviate from the LLMs' English prior in ways that render them incomprehensible to humans. Moreover, we identify several qualities essential to this evolution: pressure towards efficiency; the strength of the models backing the agents; and access to a "postmortem" stage in which agents can agree on linguistic conventions. Importantly, we observe that different conditions govern the transmission of language to new agents. Specifically, we find that agents learn new languages from usage alone, take an active role in this learning, and that while stronger models are required for novel language emergence, weaker models can learn an existing language once it has emerged. Taken together, our results indicate that current LLMs have the potential for cumulative cultural evolution -- previously attested only in humans -- with mixed populations of agents developing capacities that go beyond their lowest common denominator.

</details>


### [233] [Knowledge Distillation During Mid-Training Favors Reasoning over Factual Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01532)
*Jacqueline He,Howard Yen,Shuyue Stella Li,Margaret Li,Hanqing Zeng,Yinglong Xia,Benyu Zhang,Zhuokai Zhao,Qiang Zhang,Pang Wei Koh,Luke Zettlemoyer,Wen-tau Yih*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Switch Distillation 的新方法，通过在教师模型置信度高的 token 上进行知识蒸馏来优化自监督学习的中期训练阶段，解决了传统前向 KL 蒸馏在此阶段导致事实记忆获取放缓的问题，显著提升了推理、知识和常识能力，同时保持了高事实召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于 Logit 的知识蒸馏（KD）广泛用于通过强教师模型指导小语言模型的训练，但其在不同训练阶段的效果一致性尚不明确。特别是标准的前向 KL 蒸馏在中期训练（mid-training）阶段的表现存在异常，需要探究其背后的机制并寻找更优的训练策略。

Method: 1. **实验分析**：通过受控实验对比标准前向 KL 蒸馏与常规下一个词预测（NTP），发现前向 KD 在预训练阶段同时提升推理和事实记忆，但在中期训练阶段虽保持推理增益却延缓了事实记忆的获取。归因于教师模型在程序性数据上比知识密集型数据更自信，而学生模型早期即可获取低熵事实知识。
2. **提出 Switch Distillation**：设计一种轻量级的中期训练目标，利用教师预测熵作为路由信号，仅在教师自信的 token 上进行蒸馏；否则回退到交叉熵损失。

Result: Switch Distillation 在不同大小的教师模型中均优于现有蒸馏目标。相比标准 NTP：
- **中期训练后**：推理性能提升 1.61-1.71 倍，知识和常识性能提升 1.13-1.19 倍，同时保留 96.7-96.8% 的事实召回率。
- **后训练后**：弥合了事实召回率的差距，并保持推理性能提升 1.25-1.32 倍，知识和常识性能提升 1.13-1.20 倍。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的效果高度依赖于训练阶段。针对中期训练提出的 Switch Distillation 通过平衡教师置信度和学生知识状态，有效缓解了传统蒸馏方法的局限性，实现了推理能力、知识积累与事实记忆之间的更好平衡，且优势具有持久性。

Abstract: Logit-based knowledge distillation (KD) is used to train smaller language models (LMs) via supervision from stronger teachers, but whether its benefits are consistent across training stages remains unclear. Through controlled experiments, we find that forward Kullback-Leibler (KL) distillation--the standard KD formulation--with post-trained teachers behaves fundamentally differently during mid-training, an intermediate phase of self-supervised learning on curated corpora. Surprisingly, while forward KD simultaneously improves reasoning and factual recall during pre-training relative to standard next-token prediction (NTP), it instead slows factual recall acquisition during mid-training despite continued reasoning gains. We trace this stage dependence to an asymmetry in teacher confidence across data domains and the student's evolving knowledge state: teachers are more confident on procedural than knowledge-intensive data, while students acquire low-entropy factual knowledge earlier in training. To mitigate this imbalance, we propose Switch Distillation, a simple mid-training objective that distills on tokens where the teacher is confident, using teacher predictive entropy as a lightweight routing signal, and otherwise falls back to cross-entropy. Switch Distillation consistently outperforms existing distillation objectives across teacher sizes. Relative to standard NTP, it achieves 1.61-1.71x the reasoning performance and 1.13-1.19x the knowledge and commonsense performance while preserving 96.7-96.8% of factual recall. Crucially, these benefits persist after post-training: Switch Distillation closes the factual recall gap while maintaining 1.25-1.32x and 1.13-1.20x gains in reasoning and knowledge and commonsense, respectively.

</details>


### [234] [SDARE-Bench: Evaluating Large Language Models on Conversational Stigma Detection and Response in Dyadic and Group Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01548)
*Stephanie Fong,Yiwen Jiang,Zimu Wang,Hongxi Yang,Yaling Shen,Hiu Weh Naomi Chow,Heung Ying Lai,Xiangyu Zhao,Qingyang Xu,Zhongxing Xu,Jiahe Liu,Guilherme C. Oliveira,Vincent Lee,Zongyuan Ge,Dominic Dwyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于场景的基准测试SDARE-Bench，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在社会偏见和污名化检测及回复生成方面的表现。研究发现，LLM在群体对话中识别污名化的能力较差，且更容易生成带有污名化色彩的建议，尤其在面临群体压力时，污名化表达率高达97.5%，揭示了LLM在社会复杂语境下的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM的评估多依赖静态提示和固定格式任务，忽视了日常交流中的对话情境和受众效应。由于污名化对社会和个人有深远影响，但缺乏相关的基准测试，因此需要一种能模拟真实社交互动、评估污名化检测和开放回复生成的新评估方法。

Method: 构建了SDARE-Bench基准测试，包含1,138个二元查询和1,388个群体对话场景。通过在8个大语言模型上进行实证评估，使用一个基于1,392个人类标注回复训练的 classifier 来评估回复质量，并特别构建了群体压力设置以观察污名化表达的变化。

Result: 所有测试的8个LLM在识别污名化成分方面表现普遍不佳，尤其是在群体对话中。在开放回复生成方面，群体设置下的污名化表达显著高于二元对话，模型对污名化的抵抗力较弱，且给出的建议不够现实。在构造的群体压力场景中，污名化表达率进一步激增至平均97.5%。

Conclusion: 污名化响应是大语言模型的一个反复出现的安全漏洞，特别是在社会复杂的对话语境中。现有的LLM在处理涉及社会判断和群体互动的任务时存在显著的伦理和安全风险，亟需改进。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in advice seeking and decision making that may affect social judgements. Despite stigma's profound effects on people and communities, benchmarks remain scarce. Existing general-domain evaluations typically rely on static prompts and fixed-format tasks, overlooking conversational contexts and audience effects in everyday communication. To address these gaps, we introduce SDARE-Bench, the first scenario-based benchmark evaluating both stigma detection and open-ended response generation in LLMs, comprising 1,138 dyadic queries and 1,388 group dialogue. Empirical results across 8 LLMs consistently demonstrate poor identification of stigma components, especially in group dialogues. In open-ended response generation, stigma expression was substantially higher in group settings than in dyadic, with weaker resistance to stigma and more unrealistic advice. Responses were evaluated using a classifier trained on 1,392 human annotated responses. In constructed group pressure settings, stigma expression rates further increased to a striking average of 97.5%. Our findings identify stigma response as a recurring LLM safety vulnerability, especially in socially complex conversational contexts.

</details>


### [235] [A systematic Approach to constructing a Chance-and-Risk Matrix for Semiconductor Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01563)
*Ema Salkić,Alexander Fichtl,Philipp Ulrich,Hans Ehm,Marta Bonik,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的自动化流程，利用大语言模型从半导体公司的公开披露文件中提取、结构化并优先排序风险情报。该方法构建了知识图谱，并通过算法公式、LLM相关性调整和专家验证三层机制对风险进行评分和排名。在应用案例中，系统生成了76,207个有效项目（92.6%的有效性），其自动排名与专家判断具有较高的一致性（风险Spearman相关系数0.55，机会0.72），并识别出贸易限制为主要跨国风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对半导体供应链因地缘政治紧张、地理集中和技术快速变化而面临的日益增长的风险，解决当前缺乏可扩展系统来持续从企业公开披露中提取、结构化和优先处理风险情报的问题。

Method: 构建了一个端到端流水线：1) 检索半导体公司的文档；2) 使用大语言模型(LLMs)提取文中描述的风险和机遇；3) 将其组织为知识图谱，链接类别、来源和相关事件；4) 合并重复项并使用三层机制（算法公式 + LLM相关性调整 + 专家验证）进行排名。

Result: 应用于价值链上的五家公司，生成76,207个评分项目，独立检查显示92.6%的项目有效。自动排名与专家判断的平均Spearman相关系数分别为：风险0.55，机遇0.72。结果矩阵显示贸易限制是主要的跨公司风险。

Conclusion: 该自动化流程能有效从非结构化文本中提取高价值的风险情报，其排名结果与专家判断高度一致，为管理半导体供应链风险提供了可扩展且准确的智能支持工具。

Abstract: Semiconductor supply chains face escalating risks from geopolitical tensions, geographic concentration, and rapid technological shifts, yet no scalable system continuously extracts, structures, and prioritizes risk intelligence from public corporate disclosures. We present an end-to-end pipeline that retrieves corporate documents for semiconductor companies and uses large language models (LLMs) to extract the risks and opportunities they describe. It organizes these into a knowledge graph linking each item to its category, sources, and related events, then merges duplicates and ranks them with a three-layer mechanism combining an algorithmic formula, an LLM relevance adjustment, and expert validation. Applied to five companies across the value chain, the pipeline produces 76,207 scored items, of which an independent check finds 92.6% valid. The automated rankings match expert judgment at an average Spearman correlation of 0.55 for risks and 0.72 for opportunities, and the resulting matrices identify trade restrictions as the dominant cross-company risk.

</details>


### [236] [From Confusion to Clarity: Confusion-Aware Retrieval and Knowledge Injection for Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01564)
*Manish Gupta,Chaitanya Giri,Jayasimha Talur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需微调的框架，通过识别易混淆标签对、扩展候选集并生成区分规则，解决LLM在细粒度文本分类中难以区分语义相似标签的问题，显著提升Macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理具有大量语义相似标签的分类任务时表现不佳，因为预训练未能捕捉这些领域特定的细微差别。现有的检索Top-K候选标签方法虽减少了候选数量，但无法提供区分相似标签的有效信号。

Method: 提出的框架包含三个步骤：(1) 识别模型难以区分的标签对；(2) 将易混淆标签纳入候选集以扩展候选范围；(3) 生成针对性的规则来区分相似的候选标签。该方法无需微调，且生成的规则可迁移至更小、更便宜的模型。

Result: 在WOS、Flipkart和LEDGAR三个基准测试上，该方法相比检索基线将Macro F1提升了高达10.0个百分点；对于较小的模型（2B--20B），通过跨模型迁移，性能提升高达11.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了LLM在细粒度分类中的歧义问题，证明了无需微调的规则生成机制不仅能提升LLM性能，还能增强小模型的分类能力，具有良好的泛化性和成本效益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) struggle to classify text into taxonomies with many semantically similar labels, as the distinctions are domain-specific and not captured by pre-training. To handle large label spaces, a common approach retrieves top-$K$ candidate labels by embedding similarity and prompt the LLM to choose among them. However, top-$K$ retrieval reduces the number of candidates but does not help the model tell similar ones apart. When two similar labels both appear as candidates, the model lacks the signal to choose correctly between them. We propose a framework that (1) identifies which label pairs the model struggles to distinguish, (2) expands the candidate set to include confusable labels, and (3) generates targeted rules to differentiate between similar candidates. The framework requires no fine-tuning, and the generated rules transfer to smaller, cheaper models. On three benchmarks (WOS, Flipkart, LEDGAR), our approach improves Macro F1 by up to 10.0pp over retrieval baselines, with smaller models (2B--20B) gaining up to 11.5pp via cross-model transfer.

</details>


### [237] [From Production Traffic to Post-Training: Building a Self-Hosted LLM That Covers the Corporate Request Mix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01572)
*Olga Tsymboi,Dmitrii Stoianov,Ramil Latypov,Danil Taranets,Daniil Dryabin,Mikhail Gashkov,Viktor Zelenkovskiy,Aleksandr Fida,Gleb Alektorov,Nikita Gulyakov,Arthur Babkin,Aleksandr Medvedev,Pavel Gein,Anatolii Potapov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过GRPO专家合并与SLERP技术，将多应用流量整合至单一模型，在显著降低服务成本的同时提升了指令遵循、函数调用等关键质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驻留限制迫使企业自建LLM；随着新模型不断引入且旧模型未退役，GPU资源池被碎片化，导致服务成本高企。

Method: 1. 基于生产错误分析，从指令遵循、函数调用和内部任务分布三个维度缩小质量差距；2. 采用离线基准测试并结合确定性验证器或校准LLM裁判进行评分；3. 避免联合优化带来的跨域奖励干扰，为每个维度训练独立的GRPO专家；4. 通过两阶段SLERP方法合并各专家模型。

Result: 在非推理模式下，该方案在内部Arena（69.6 vs 65.8）、指令遵循（0.85 vs 0.83）和函数调用（0.79 vs 0.77）等指标上均优于参数量约7倍的基线模型，同时通用对话基准性能提升。模型承载了50%的平台流量（每月1.16亿次请求），大幅降低了服务成本。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了GPU资源碎片化问题，实现了高吞吐量下的低成本、高质量LLM服务部署。

Abstract: Data-residency constraints force enterprises to self-host LLMs, but continuous adoption of newer models without decommissioning their predecessors expands the serving fleet, fragmenting a finite GPU pool. We consolidate traffic from over 200 internal applications onto a single model by closing quality gaps identified through production error analysis along three axes: instruction following, function-calling, and internal task distribution. Quality is tracked by offline benchmarks stratified to production traffic and scored by deterministic verifiers or calibrated LLM judges. Rather than optimising all objectives jointly, which introduces cross-domain reward interference, we train a separate GRPO expert per axis and merge them via two-stage SLERP. Each expert's reward exposes a distinct failure mode, namely semantic collapse, over-calling, and verbosity hacking, each requiring a domain-specific fix. In non-reasoning mode the recipe surpasses a ${\sim}7\times$ larger by total parameters baseline on the in-house Arena with 69.6 to 65.8, instruction following with 0.85 to 0.83, and function-calling with 0.79 to 0.77, while lifting general dialogue benchmarks. The model absorbs 50% of platform traffic, 116M requests per month, at a fraction of the serving cost.

</details>


### [238] [Scaling Near-Optimal SFT-RL Annotation Budget Allocation from Small to Large LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01573)
*Jingtan Wang,Arun Verma,Xiaoqiang Lin,Zhengyuan Liu,Nancy F. Chen,Daniela Rus,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“近最优区域”的框架，用于在大型语言模型后训练阶段分配监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的固定标注预算。研究发现该近优区域具有宽泛性、随模型规模扩大而变宽，且能从小型代理模型可靠地迁移至大型目标模型，从而通过小规模实验即可确定高效且通用的资源分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型后训练中SFT与RL之间固定标注预算分配的开放性问题。现有研究仅描述大致趋势，缺乏原则性的分配框架，且未考察最优比例是否在不同模型规模间迁移。

Method: 将问题框架化为“近最优性”，即不寻求单一的最优SFT-RL比例，而是界定性能峰值指定容差范围内的“近最优区域”。通过实证分析不同容差下的区域宽度、模型规模影响及跨模型迁移能力，并对比不同任务、模型家族及RL方法（偏好-based off-policy 和 reward-supervision on-policy）。

Result: 1. 近最优区域即使在极小容差（2-10%）下也较宽；2. 该区域随模型规模增大而变宽；3. 从小型代理模型到大型目标模型的近优区域具有可靠的迁移性；4. 结果在不同任务、模型族和RL方法中保持一致；5. SFT与RL数据标注成本的不对称性会移动近优区域。

Conclusion: 通过小型代理模型实验即可识别可迁移的近最优区域，无需进行耗时的全尺度搜索，为LLM后训练中的资源分配提供了实用且高效的策略。

Abstract: How to divide a fixed annotation budget between supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) during LLM post-training remains an open problem. Existing work characterizes only broad trends (e.g., SFT dominates in low-data regimes), lacks a principled allocation framework, and does not examine whether the optimal ratio transfers across model sizes. We frame this problem in terms of near-optimality: rather than seeking a single optimal SFT-RL ratio, we characterize the near-optimal region, the set of allocations within a specified tolerance of peak performance. Empirically, this region is wide even for small tolerances (2-10%), widens with model scale, and transfers reliably from small proxy models to large target models. This yields a practical strategy: small proxy-model experiments suffice to identify a transferable near-optimal region, eliminating the need for exhaustive large-scale search. Our results hold consistently across tasks, model families, and both preference-based off-policy and reward-supervision on-policy RL methods. We further analyze how the asymmetry in annotation costs between SFT and RL data shifts the near-optimal region.

</details>


### [239] [Closing Cost-Quality Gap in Document VLMs: Difficulty-Aware Data Curation and Quality-Adjusted Deployment Economics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01575)
*Maksim Evdokimov,Matvey Ivanov,Dmitrii Tsiupin,Olga Tsymboi,Anatolii Potapov,Aleksandr Ivanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合专家视觉语言模型（MoE VLM）的文档理解系统，通过内部数据微调，在单张H100 GPU上实现高效部署，显著降低合规行业结构化字段提取成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决受监管行业中从海量文档中提取结构化字段的高昂成本问题。现有方案存在定制OCR覆盖范围有限、隐私限制阻碍外部模型使用、以及高质量开源VLM服务成本高于人工标注等痛点。

Method: 构建并部署一个35B总参数、3B激活参数的MoE VLM系统。该方法利用“难度感知”管道对内部生产数据与开放域文档进行混合微调，以增强布局多样性、事实可提取性和跨模型一致性；通过提示工程适配异构工作流，并在单张H100 GPU上进行推理服务。

Result: 该模型性能优于所有可部署的非推理基线模型（最大达一个数量级）。质量调整的成本分析显示：相比人工标注基准，预期成本降低超过80%；相比最佳竞争开源模型，成本降低超过50%；而更大规模的基线模型在经济上不可行。

Conclusion: 基于MoE架构的轻量化VLM结合特定领域数据微调，是在严格隐私和成本约束下实现高精度、低成本文档智能处理的可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Extracting structured fields from hundreds of millions of documents annually remains costly in regulated industries: bespoke OCR cascades cover only a fraction of workflows, privacy rules preclude external models, and existing open-source VLMs that clear quality thresholds cost more to serve than human annotation. We present a deployed document-understanding system built on a Mixture-of-Experts VLM (35B total, 3B active), fine-tuned on in-house production data mixed with open-domain documents curated by a Difficulty-Aware pipeline for layout diversity, fact-extractability, and cross-model consistency. Fitting on a single H100 and serving heterogeneous workflows via prompting, the model leads all deployable (non-reasoning) baselines up to an order of magnitude larger. A quality-adjusted cost analysis, with confirmation and correction costs calibrated from production telemetry, shows it reduces expected costs by over 80% against the human baseline and by more than 50% against the best competing open-source model, while larger baselines remain economically unviable.

</details>


### [240] [StudentSim: Training LLM-based Student Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01591)
*Ke Yang,Chenglong Wang,Michel Galley,Chandan Singh,Jeevana Priya Inala,ChengXiang Zhai,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StudentSim框架，通过池化训练和个性化微调构建学生模拟器，解决AI导师自适应指导数据稀缺问题；引入StudentSimEval评估协议，在棋类、写作和数学领域证明其优于GPT-5.4等基线模型，并成功用于强化学习训练出更精准、个性化的AI导师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI导师需适应学生个体差异，但真实收集“何种指导对何种学生有效”的数据成本高且稀疏；现有学生模拟器（状态追踪模型或LLM角色扮演）存在局限：前者难以处理解释/修正，后者无法可靠匹配被模仿学生的能力水平。

Method: 提出StudentSim训练框架：先进行池化训练（pooled training），再进行每名学生个性化 specialization（specialization），将稀疏的单人数据转化为个体化模拟器。同时提出StudentSimEval标准化评估协议，覆盖60名学生在国际象棋、二语英语写作和数学三个领域，使用去标识化的公开学习者数据集。

Result: 在三个领域均超越GPT-5.4和Maia2。在国际象棋中，StudentSim的行为保真度(F=0.51)和指导响应性(R=0.91)显著高于GPT-5.4 (F=0.23, R=0.72) 和 Maia2 (F=0.45, R=0.27)。作为概念验证，使用该模拟器作为奖励模型训练的AI导师，经专家人类评价，比无RL基线和基于GPT-5.4模拟器的导师更准确、引导更好、更个性化。

Conclusion: StudentSim能有效生成高保真、高响应性的个性化学生模拟器，为AI导师自适应优化提供可靠信号来源，具有实际应用价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: AI tutors are most useful when they adapt to each student's strengths, weaknesses, and preferred guidance, but evidence about which guidance works for which student is sparse, slow, and costly to collect from real learners. Student simulators can provide this signal as a proxy, yet existing approaches are limited: state-tracking models fit student behavior but struggle to process explanations or corrections, while LLM role-play follows guidance fluently but does not reliably match the competence of the student being imitated. We present StudentSim, a training framework that turns sparse per-student data into individualized simulators through pooled training followed by per-student specialization. The resulting simulators both mirror a student's own responses and update them under tutor guidance. We also introduce StudentSimEval, a standardized protocol covering 60 students across chess, second-language English writing, and mathematics, using public learner datasets with de-identified records shared for research. StudentSimEval measures behavioral fidelity (F), or how well a simulator matches a student's responses, and guidance responsiveness (R), or how readily it updates under tutor guidance, with all methods fit and evaluated on the same records. Across all three domains, StudentSim outperforms GPT-5.4 on both metrics. In chess, StudentSim reaches F=0.51 and R=0.91, compared with 0.23 and 0.72 for GPT-5.4 and 0.45 and 0.27 for Maia2. As a proof of concept, using StudentSim as a reward model for tutor reinforcement learning produces a chess tutor that expert humans rate as more accurate, better-guided, and more personalized than a no-RL baseline and a tutor trained against a GPT-5.4 simulator reward. Code is available at https://github.com/microsoft/StudentSim.

</details>


### [241] [The Rise of Verbal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01597)
*Kshitij Tayal,Arun Sharma,Genta Indra Winata,Anirban Das,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“言语强化学习”（Verbal Reinforcement Learning, VRL）这一统一范式，旨在利用自然语言作为反馈通道来提升语言智能体。文章围绕反馈生效的时机和修改对象构建了三支柱分类体系：语言作为接地信号、 deliberative 反馈和学习信号，并综述了相关代表性工作、子类别及挑战机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言正成为提升语言智能体的主要反馈渠道，能够以人和现代语言模型均可理解的形式传达意图、偏好和因果结构。目前缺乏对该范式的统一论述，因此需要建立系统的理论框架来组织该领域。

Method: 提出VRL统一框架，基于两个维度（反馈生效时机与修改内容）将领域划分为三大支柱：(1) 语言作为接地信号（定义任务目标、状态和奖励结构）；(2) 语言作为推理性反馈（在测试时引导推理，无需更新参数）；(3) 语言作为学习信号（通过训练塑造模型参数）。对各支柱下的代表性工作进行综合梳理，区分关键子类别，并阐述语言在塑造智能体行为中的具体作用。

Result: 构建了一个清晰的VRL分类体系，展示了言语强化如何重塑智能体开发过程。通过对不同方法的归纳和对比，明确了自然语言在不同阶段对智能体行为和参数更新的具体影响机制。

Conclusion: 该分类体系不仅揭示了言语强化在智能体发展中的作用，还定义了构建更强大且对齐的智能体所面临的挑战与未来机遇，为后续研究提供了统一的理论基础和方向指引。

Abstract: Natural language is emerging as a primary feedback channel for improving language agents, capable of conveying intent, preferences, and causal structure in forms interpretable by both humans and modern language models. We call this paradigm Verbal Reinforcement Learning (VRL) and offer the first unified account of it. We organize the field around a single axis, \textit{when} verbal feedback takes effect in an agent's lifecycle and \textit{what} it modifies, yielding three pillars: (1) \textbf{Language as Grounding Signal}, where language defines the task itself by specifying goals, states, and reward structures; (2) \textbf{Language as Deliberative Feedback}, where natural language guides reasoning at test time without the need to update model parameters; (3) \textbf{Language as Learning Signal}, where language-based feedback shapes model parameters through training. Within each pillar, we synthesize representative work, distinguish key subcategories of approaches, and outline the distinct role language plays in shaping agent behavior. Together, this taxonomy shows how verbal reinforcement is reshaping agent development, while also defining the challenges and opportunities for building more capable and aligned agents.

</details>


### [242] [CordisBench: Can Language Models Reason About Component Lifecycles in Dynamic Agent Harnesses?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01600)
*Damien Sileo,Dimitri Kachler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CordisBench，一个包含1,200道题目的基准测试，用于评估语言模型在动态代理插件生命周期推理方面的能力。研究发现，虽然模型在小系统中表现良好，但随着交互复杂度增加，可靠性显著下降；尽管通过增加推理努力可以部分恢复性能，但成本高昂。此外，研究证明存在一种独立的有限参考语义，能准确预测执行结果，从而为降低推理成本提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态代理框架允许语言模型修改影响其自身执行的软件，这种灵活性带来了新的推理负担：局部插件更改会通过依赖关系和清理过程产生连锁反应。现有的评估缺乏针对这一特定“生命周期推理”问题的标准化基准。

Method: 作者构建了CordisBench基准测试，结合受控的形式化设置与基于Cordis运行时（管理组件依赖和清理）的实际程序执行。任务包括识别受影响组件、预测指定拆除顺序后的状态、确定在所有或部分顺序下成立的条件，以及选择成功的重新配置方案。研究评估了三种面向效率的模型，在不同数量的相关交互（2至32个）下，使用确定性任务特定评分进行测量。

Result: 模型通常能很好地处理小规模系统，但随着相关交互数量增加，其可靠性降低，特别是在预测最终状态和跨拆除顺序推理时。虽然增加推理努力可使某些模型获得显著提升，但代价高昂（例如GPT-5.6 Luna在16次交互子集中每道题消耗近3,000个推理令牌）。然而，对于受控实例，存在一种独立的有限参考语义，其预测结果与Cordis执行在所有528个可执行问题的观察和操作结果上完全一致。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在处理复杂的动态代理依赖推理时面临可扩展性和效率挑战。虽然增加计算资源可以提升性能，但利用已验证的有限参考语义作为替代方案，可以在保证正确性的前提下避免高昂的推理成本，这为解决此类复杂系统推理问题提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Dynamic agent harnesses let language models change the software that shapes their own execution. This flexibility brings a new reasoning burden: a local plugin change can propagate through dependencies and cleanup. We introduce CordisBench, a 1,200-question benchmark of this lifecycle reasoning. It combines a controlled formal setting with programs executed against Cordis, a runtime that manages component dependencies and cleanup, and asks models to identify affected components, predict state after a specified teardown order, determine which conditions hold under all or some orders, and choose reconfigurations that succeed when executed. Across these tasks, we evaluate three efficiency-oriented models at low reasoning effort with 2, 4, 8, 16, 24, or 32 relevant interactions, using deterministic task-specific scoring. Models usually handle small systems well but grow less reliable as more interactions become relevant, especially when predicting final state and when reasoning across teardown orders. Additional inference effort recovers marked gains for some models. The cost is nontrivial: on our 16-interaction subset, GPT-5.6 Luna uses nearly 3,000 reasoning tokens per question at medium effort. For these controlled instances, that cost is avoidable: an independent finite reference semantics agrees with Cordis execution on every observation and action outcome used for scoring across all 528 executable questions.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Scores: Understanding LLM-as-a-Judge Mechanisms in Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01604)
*Himil Vasava,Ming Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过八种攻击扰动和因果追踪等方法，机制性地揭示了LLM评估器（Themis和Prometheus）对NLG质量评分的内部工作原理。发现评估分为两阶段：浅层进行局部错误比较并路由，深层MLP整合信号并生成评分。微调并非从零构建，而是优化现有架构，具体表现为抑制浅层MLP贡献并提前结晶深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于大语言模型的评估器广泛用于NLG质量评分和训练信号，但其内部评分机制仍缺乏深入理解。本研究旨在通过机械性探究揭开这一“黑盒”。

Method: 1. 构建包含可读性和充分性维度的八种攻击扰动分类法；2. 生成带有受控误差强度和显式词级修改映射的配对干净/损坏摘要；3. 在Themis (Llama-3-8B) 和 Prometheus (Mistral-7B) 上应用因果追踪、logit-lens词汇投影和注意力头剔除等四种实验方法；4. 使用同规模基础模型（Llama-3-8B）作为对照以隔离微调效应。

Result: 评估器执行结构化、连贯的两阶段流程：第15层以下，注意力机制执行局部错误比较并将结果路由至最终输入位置；第15层以上，MLP级联整合信号并写入评分，决策在第26层（Themis）或第25层（Prometheus）的残差流中急剧结晶。基础模型复现了路由架构和结晶现象，但未表现出阶段分离。

Conclusion: 微调并未从头构建评估管道，而是塑造了现有的底层架构。具体而言，微调实现了两个特定机制：一是在最后位置抑制第15层以下的MLP贡献，二是将结晶深度提前两层。研究代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: LLM-based evaluators of natural language generation (NLG) quality are widely deployed as scoring tools and as automated training signals, yet the internal procedure by which they assign a rating remains poorly understood. We investigate this procedure mechanistically through an eight-attack perturbation taxonomy across the Readability and Adequacy dimensions of NLG quality, a generation pipeline that produces paired clean and corrupt summaries with controlled error intensity and explicit token-level modification maps, and a four-experiment battery of causal tracing, logit-lens vocabulary projection, and attention-head knockout applied to Themis (Llama-3-8B) and Prometheus (Mistral-7B). Both evaluators implement a structured, coherent evaluation pipeline operating in two stages: below layer 15, attention performs local error comparison and routes the result to the final input position; above it, the MLP cascade integrates the signal and writes the rating, with the decision crystallizing in the residual stream at a sharp late layer (L = 26 on Themis, L = 25 on Prometheus). Furthermore, a base-model control at the same scale (Llama-3-8B) reproduces the routing architecture and crystallization but not the stage separation, isolating the two mechanisms that fine-tuning specifically installs, suppression of below-L15 MLP contribution at the last position and a two-layer advance of the crystallization depth, indicating that fine-tuning sculpts an existing substrate rather than building the pipeline from scratch. We release the source code and data at https://github.com/himil-v/judge-mech

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Intelligent Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00181)
*Kalgi Gandhi,Minal Bhise*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出WACI-HJ算法，通过工作负载感知和预测优化边缘系统的哈希连接性能，显著减少缓存行读取并提升查询执行速度、CPU/RAM/I/O利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模边缘系统中因设备资源（处理、内存、带宽）受限导致的边缘查询处理挑战，特别是针对现有CI-HJ算法在实时处理和缓存行扫描效率上的不足。

Method: 提出工作负载感知列印哈希连接（WACI-HJ），包含两个阶段：1. WACI-HJ生成阶段（预处理、预测、分块与哈希），基于预测的工作负载预先计算分桶；2. 查询处理与资源利用阶段，处理查询及CPU、RAM、I/O资源管理。

Result: 在基准数据集和真实世界智能交通数据集上，缓存行读取减少54%，查询执行时间提升10%；CPU、RAM和I/O效率分别提升1%、38%和49%；对缩放数据和偏斜数据均有效。

Conclusion: WACI-HJ能有效加速边缘查询处理，特别适用于实时交通分析、拥堵管理和路由等场景，并可扩展至其他领域。

Abstract: The number of edge devices in large-scale edge systems is rapidly increasing. Edge devices have limited processing power, memory, and network bandwidth, making resource utilization and data management during edge query processing challenging. Joins are among the costliest database operations in terms of time and resources. The State-of-the-Art edge query processing, Column Imprint-Hash Join CI-HJ, addresses this challenge using equi-height binning to accelerate hash joins. However, it lacks efficiency in real-time processing and scans unnecessary cachelines. This paper presents Workload Aware Column Imprint-Hash Join WACI-HJ, which uses a workload-aware approach to accelerate hash joins. Predicting the upcoming query workload in advance further improves its suitability for real-time edge query processing. WACI-HJ comprises two phases: WACI-HJ Generation Phase, including Pre-processing, Prediction, and Blocking and Hashing modules to compute bins based on the predicted workload before query arrival, and Query Processing and Resource Utilization, which handles query processing and CPU, RAM, and I/O utilization. Evaluations on a benchmark dataset and a real-world Smart Transportation dataset show a 54% reduction in cachelines read and 10% improved query execution time. The proposed technique is effective for both scaled and skewed data. Although PCR is an indirect measure of energy consumption, the work also directly measures energy consumption through energy-efficiency experiments. WACI-HJ shows 1%, 38%, and 49% gain in CPU, RAM, and I/O, respectively. Optimizing cache usage and query execution speeds up real-time traffic analysis, congestion management, and routing in Smart Transportation. Additionally, this technology can be applied to other domains to accelerate edge query processing.

</details>


### [245] [Bounded, Indeterminate, or a Bug: A Condition-Aware Oracle for Differential Testing of SQL Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00381)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种基于精确有理值和算法条件数的浮点聚合测试预言机，克服了传统引擎对比法的缺陷；通过误差界限分析揭示ClickHouse等单遍算法在方差计算上的不可测性，并验证了该方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分数据库测试（比较不同引擎结果）对浮点聚合是不合理的，因为浮点运算不具备结合律，导致引擎间合法差异被误判为Bug；现有实践依赖启发式epsilon或完全避免浮点，缺乏严谨的理论依据。

Method: 以存储双精度的精确有理值为“地面真值”，将每次差异分类为精确、有界或不确定；建立相对误差与算法条件数关系的理论模型 rel_err <= C_A(n,u) * kappa_f^p，定义可测边界 kappa*_{f,A}；通过分析八个引擎的指数恢复其算法类型。

Result: 1. 成功通过测量指数恢复各引擎算法（如ClickHouse为单遍算法p≈2.05，其他为Welford等）；2. ClickHouse因使用单遍算法，在条件数较高时方差计算出现严重错误（标准差为零、NaN相关系数），而Welford实现保持精确；3. 随机搜索360个测试未发现异常，证明预言机健全。

Conclusion: 浮点聚合的可测试性取决于引擎算法而非查询本身；对于高条件数场景（如时间戳、传感器数据），单遍算法存在根本性的数值不稳定问题，而基于精确有理值的预言机能有效区分Bug与舍入误差，为数据库浮点测试提供了可靠标准。

Abstract: Differential database testing compares results across engines and calls a discrepancy a bug. For floating-point aggregates this is unsound: engines legitimately disagree because floating-point arithmetic is not associative. Practice patches this with an epsilon; the leading oracles avoid floating point entirely. We give the oracle this practice lacks, and show its decisive quantity is not the query but the engine's algorithm. Ground truth is the exact rational value of the stored doubles -- arithmetic, not another engine -- and each discrepancy is classified exact, bounded, or indeterminate. The relative error of an aggregate f under an algorithm A obeys rel_err <= C_A(n,u) * kappa_f^p, so the testability boundary, beyond which no oracle can separate a bug from rounding, is kappa*_{f,A} = (1/C_A)^{1/p}. SUM and AVG are the linear case p=1; variance is p=2 for the one-pass algorithm and p=1 for Welford. Across eight engines in four classes the measured exponent recovers each algorithm, and ClickHouse is the lone one-pass engine (p=2.05); engine-wide, it returns zero standard deviation, NaN correlation and wrong-sign regression, while every other engine stays exact and the vendor ships the Welford fix. Its variance is untestable at a condition number 10^6 below SUM's, which ordinary storage conventions (epoch-nanosecond timestamps, tight sensors) cross -- there ClickHouse errs by 2100%. A randomised hunt of 360 tests finds zero anomalies, evidence the oracle is sound. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [246] [Time-Decayed Vector Search in the Rhythm of TANGO: Jointly Modeling Semantic Similarity and Temporal Freshness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00548)
*Jiuqi Wei,Qiyao Luo,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出时间衰减向量搜索（TDVS），通过Score-Preserving Temporal Reduction (STR) 和 Chronos 框架解决语义相似性与时间新鲜度结合的问题，并设计 TANGO 层级图索引实现高效查询与构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统向量搜索仅依赖固定评分函数的语义相似度，但在许多应用中，相关性随时间演变，需要引入“时间新鲜度”作为额外信号。现有方法难以有效结合这两者，且缺乏原生支持时间衰减的高效索引结构。

Method: 1. 形式化定义时间衰减向量搜索（TDVS）；2. 设计 Score-Preserving Temporal Reduction (STR)，使现有最大内积搜索索引能直接支持 TDVS；3. 提出 Chronos 框架，引入 Query-Orthogonal TimeLift 保持数据-数据几何及查询-数据分数/排名不变；4. 基于 Chronos 开发 TANGO，一种层级图索引，采用分层 TimeLift 几何以保留底层时间局部性并增强上层长程语义连接，支持精确 TDVS 评分遍历、时间因子缓存及在线插入。

Result: TANGO 相比最先进的基于图的竞争方案，查询吞吐量最高提升 3.5 倍，索引构建速度提升 4.05 倍；在不同时间设置下均保持优势，并具备高效的在线插入能力，展现出鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: TANGO 通过结合语义相似性与时间新鲜度，提供了高效、可扩展且支持动态更新的时间衰减向量搜索解决方案，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Vector search typically measures relevance through semantic similarity under a fixed scoring function. However, in a growing range of applications, relevance may evolve over time, making temporal freshness an additional signal beyond semantic similarity. In this paper, we formalize time-decayed vector search (TDVS), which incorporates continuous temporal decay into the search objective so that relevance is jointly determined by semantic similarity and temporal freshness. We design Score-Preserving Temporal Reduction (STR) that enables existing Maximum Inner Product Search indexes to directly support TDVS. We further present Chronos, a TDVS-native framework that derives an exact metric formulation and introduces Query-Orthogonal TimeLift to control data--data geometry while preserving all query--data scores and rankings. Building on Chronos, we propose TANGO, a hierarchical graph index that adopts layer-specific TimeLift geometries to preserve temporal locality at the base layer while strengthening long-range semantic connectivity in upper layers. TANGO traverses the hierarchy using the exact TDVS score, caches temporal factors to reduce computation, and supports efficient online insertion. Extensive experiments show that TANGO achieves up to 3.5$\times$ higher query throughput and 4.05$\times$ faster index construction than state-of-the-art graph-based competitors. TANGO also maintains its advantage over all competitors across diverse temporal settings and enables efficient online insertion, demonstrating its robustness and practicality.

</details>


### [247] [Efficient discovery of unique column combinations on disk-resident data with limited memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00783)
*Xiaolong Wan,Xixian Han*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出DUD算法，通过超图理论和部分差异集生成，在有限内存下高效发现磁盘驻留数据中的唯一列组合（UCCs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法因高内存消耗和计算成本，难以处理大规模磁盘驻留数据的UCC发现任务。

Method: 基于UCC发现与横贯超图的关系，仅生成部分差异集构建超图；通过元组配对比较生成完整有用差异集；利用新定理直接判定候选项，减少验证数量；采用基于哈希的批量验证策略，仅需少量内存维护元组。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，DUD能高效发现磁盘驻留数据中的UCCs，具有低内存消耗和高效率。

Conclusion: DUD算法有效解决了大规模磁盘数据UCC发现的内存和计算瓶颈，为数据配置分析提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The discovery of unique column combinations (UCCs) is a core task in data profiling, describing the key constraints of a table. The existing algorithms cannot deal with large-scale disk-resident data well due to high memory consumption and computational cost. In this paper, a novel DUD algorithm is developed to efficiently discover UCCs on disk-resident data with limited memory, which is inspired by the relationship between UCC discovery and transversal hypergraph. Rather than complete difference set generation of quadratic complexity, DUD only generates partial difference sets for hypergraph construction, followed by minimal hitting set enumeration to generate candidates and a validation process. DUD devises a strategy to generate full useful difference sets by pairwise comparisons of tuples having the same values with respect to some selected attributes. A novel theorem is developed and proved in this paper to report the candidates including the selected attributes as true UCCs directly without validation, which reduces the number of candidates to be validated significantly. A hash-based batch validation strategy is devised to validate a set of candidates on the relation instance, which only needs to maintain a small number of tuples in memory at a time. The extensive experimental results, conducted on synthetic and real-life data sets, show that DUD can discover UCCs on disk-resident data with high efficiency and low memory consumption.

</details>


### [248] [Relational-Core Graph Analytics Querying graphs at SQL scale, and why the node/edge model is a performance tax, not a truer picture of connected data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01525)
*Gene Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文反驳了“图分析必须专用引擎”的传统观点，提出基于列式关系型数据库（如ClickHouse、Databricks）结合图查询语言（Cypher）即可高效处理图数据。通过ClickGraph和DeltaGraph系统，直接将图查询转化为原生SQL执行，无需数据导入或独立集群。基准测试显示其性能比Neo4j高2-4个数量级，且具备更好的可扩展性和优化空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“关系型系统不适合连接型数据”的固有假设，证明企业实际运行的图分析工作负载在关系型引擎上同样高效甚至更优，旨在消除对专用图引擎的依赖，降低基础设施复杂度。

Method: 提出ClickGraph和DeltaGraph系统，将Cypher图查询语言直接翻译为底层关系型模式（表、列、外键）的原生SQL，在ClickHouse、Databricks或湖仓文件上就地执行。该方法避免节点/边属性图的冗余编码开销，利用现有关系结构进行查询重构。

Result: 1. 性能优势：在LDBC社交网络基准测试及同行发布的基准中，列式关系引擎性能超越Neo4j等原生图引擎2至4个数量级；
2. 扩展性：在内存图引擎失效的大规模数据场景下仍能良好扩展；
3. 可优化性：输出为标准SQL，便于查询重写和引擎扩展。

Conclusion: 节点/边属性图并非连接数据的更忠实模型，而是对已有关系显式编码的重述，重构过程带来纯开销。基于关系型数据库的图查询方案在性能、扩展性和灵活性上均优于专用图引擎，是企业级图分析的有效替代方案。

Abstract: A durable assumption holds that graph analytics requires a purpose-built graph engine, and that relational systems are ill-suited to connected data. We argue the opposite for the workloads enterprises actually run. A columnar relational engine fronted by a graph query language matches or exceeds native graph engines on analytical graph queries, and - decisively - scales past the point where in-memory graph engines fail. We further argue that the node/edge property graph is not a more faithful model of connected data but a re-encoding of relationships that already exist explicitly in relational tables; reconstructing them at query time is pure overhead. We present ClickGraph and its Databricks-dialect sibling DeltaGraph, systems that translate Cypher directly onto the native relational schema - the tables, columns, and foreign keys as they already exist - and execute in place on ClickHouse, Databricks, or in-process on lakehouse files, with no import and no separate cluster. Because the output is ordinary SQL, an underperforming query is an open optimization surface: it can be rewritten, and the engine itself extended. We support the argument with a peer system's own published benchmark, in which a columnar engine outruns Neo4j by two-to-four orders of magnitude, and with reproducible measurements across the LDBC Social Network Benchmark suite.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HyperWorld，通过对比实验证明在文本世界模型中，使用实体为中心的超边（hyperedge）序列化结构比原始观察、独立句子或二元三元组更能提升小中型模型的泛化能力和规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在文本环境中，状态描述的序列化结构对语言模型代理学习符号动作效应及预测环境动态的影响，特别是解决当前该领域研究不足的现状。

Method: 构建受控研究框架HyperWorld，比较四种状态表示形式：原始观察、独立句子、成对三元组和实体为中心的超边单元。所有变体使用相同的训练目标（预测符号效应或判断动作不可行），并在不同模型规模、数据预算及分布内/外测试场景下进行评估。

Result: 1. 超边序列化在0.5B-1.5B模型及分布外（OOD）测试中表现最佳；2. 大模型缩小了各方法间的差距，且在分布内精确匹配上二元三元组可能略优；3. 超边在分布外事实F1得分和小到中等规模模型的可执行性检测与效应预测权衡上优势显著；4. 在下游贪婪规划任务中，超边世界模型取得了最高的成功率。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织（如超边序列化）是学习型符号世界模型一种简单且有效的归纳偏置，尤其在模型容量受限或测试环境与训练环境存在分布差异时效果更为突出。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [250] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: I-CARE提出了一种用于形式化研究生成式机器遗忘中“干扰”现象的方法论，提供了标准化的任务定义、指标和报告模板，并通过开源框架验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式机器遗忘进展迅速，但遗忘目标概念时导致的语义相关概念意外退化（即干扰）缺乏系统性的表征和不一致的评估标准。

Method: 提出了I-CARE方法论，将干扰作为核心研究对象，定义了正式的任务、指标和结果报告模板；未提出新的基准或算法，而是侧重于建立系统化、可复现的研究框架，并提供了开源软件实现及可视化界面。

Result: 通过最先进的算法和数据集进行了可行性演示，结果表明I-CARE能够跨多种遗忘设置对干扰模式进行有意义的分析，证明了该框架的实际适用性。

Conclusion: I-CARE为生成式机器遗忘中的干扰研究提供了标准化和可复现的分析工具，有助于在模型和算法演进的背景下获得长期的科学见解。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [251] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究具有随机到达时间的多物品有限期产能限制批量问题，提出基于马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP）的建模方法及遗传算法（GA），实验证明该算法在保持接近最优解的同时显著提升了求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决需求数量确定但到达时间随机的多物品产能限制批量问题，需同时考虑产能竞争、特定需求的积压以及依赖分配的库存动态，并应对随机时机带来的计算复杂度激增。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），包含状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单周期成本函数；2. 通过对比确定性对应案例（使用最可能到达期）量化随机性的计算影响；3. 提出遗传算法（GA）搜索可行的状态反馈策略，并在DTMDP转移模型下精确评估各策略。

Result: 1. 随机时机显著增加了状态数、转移数、求解时间和内存压力；2. GA在330个基准实例上平均优化差距约为3.44%；3. 在90个困难实例中，优化差距保持在5%以下，且在95%置信水平下平均加速比为6.89±1.41；4. 对于无法精确求解的实例，利用经验Bellman时间回归估算缺失的精确分辨率时间并外推GA加速比。

Conclusion: 所提出的遗传算法能有效处理随机到达时间带来的高维状态空间问题，在可计算的范围内保持高精度，并为不可直接精确求解的大规模实例提供高效的近似解决方案及性能预测手段。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [252] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于累积轮次的风险评估框架，用于检测针对老年人的多轮对话诈骗。通过构建包含投资、慈善和技术支持场景的多轮对话数据集，并对Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2等小型语言模型进行微调，实现了在资源受限环境下对诈骗风险的增量式动态监测。其中Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2表现出较强的性能，证明了紧凑模型在隐私保护和端侧欺诈防护中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
