*Hodong Lee,Sanghee Park,Dohoon Ryu,Jungwhan Kim,Junyeob Kim,Soyoon Kim,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniEvaluator 是一个统一的评估框架，解决了多模态基础模型（文本、图像、视频、音频）评估工具链不兼容的问题。它通过单一接口连接现有的推理引擎和评估库，支持超过一千个基准测试，提供可复现的记录、跨模型比较仪表板、联邦模式共享 GPU 资源，以及一个轻量级 CPU 运行的验证器以稳定评分并降低商业 LLM 裁判的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管针对文本、图像、视频和音频等单一模态已有优秀的评估工具包，但这些工具的推理引擎、提示约定和指标实现互不兼容。这导致实践者必须为每个工具链维护独立的环境，并且难以在不同工具之间比较结果。研究人员在自身模型开发中遇到了这一痛点，因此需要一种能够整合现有资源的统一方案。

Method: OmniEvaluator 采用高层集成策略，而非重新实现基准测试。主要方法包括：1) 暴露四个推理后端和四个评估框架，通过单一接口访问超过一千个基准测试；2) 将每次运行记录为包含完整配置的艺术品（artifact），以确保精确复现；3) 结果汇入共享仪表板以支持跨模型比较；4) 引入联邦模式，允许并发评估共享 GPU 推理服务器；5) 内置一个小型验证器，可在 CPU 上运行，用于解决因配置不匹配导致的基于规则评分波动问题，其表现与成本高昂的商业 LLM 裁判相当但无 API 费用。

Result: 该系统的代码、演示视频和仪表板已公开可用（https://github.com/naver-ai/omni-evaluator）。系统成功实现了多模态评估的统一化，提供了稳定的评分机制，降低了评估的基础设施成本和复杂性，并促进了不同模型间的公平比较。

Conclusion: OmniEvaluator 提供了一个高效、统一且可复现的多模态基础模型评估平台。它通过整合现有资源而非重复造轮子，解决了评估工具碎片化的问题，并通过联邦计算和轻量级验证器优化了资源利用和评分稳定性，为多模态 AI 研究提供了标准化的评估基础设施。

Abstract: Building an omni-modal foundation model means evaluating it across text, image, video, and audio. Excellent evaluation toolkits exist for each modality, but their inference engines, prompt conventions, and metric implementations are mutually incompatible, so practitioners end up maintaining separate environments for every toolchain and still struggle to compare results across them. OmniEvaluator grew out of this need in our own model development: rather than reimplementing benchmarks, it connects existing inference engines and curated evaluation libraries at a higher level, exposing four inference backends, four evaluation frameworks, and over a thousand benchmarks through a single interface. Every run is recorded as an artifact capturing the full configuration for exact reproduction, and results flow into a shared dashboard for cross-model comparison. A federated mode shares GPU inference servers across concurrent evaluations, and a built-in verifier, small enough to run on CPU, keeps its score stable across engines and prompts where rule-based scoring fluctuates under configuration mismatch, matching cost-efficient commercial LLM judges without their recurring API cost. The system, demo video, and dashboard are publicly available. (https://github.com/naver-ai/omni-evaluator)

</details>


### [360] [Automated Event Log Generation from Unstructured Text Using Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01320)
*Maximilian Seeth,Gabriel Marques Tavares,Daniel Schuster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用微调的大语言模型（LLMs）作为自动化数据翻译器，将非结构化文本资源（如事件工单、手册）转化为结构化的过程挖掘事件日志，解决了传统方法依赖专家手动创建日志的高成本瓶颈。实验表明，微调方法在提取高保真事件日志方面显著优于少样本或零样本提示技术，为扩展过程挖掘的应用范围提供了有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程挖掘（PM）的有效性依赖于高质量的结构化事件日志，但现有日志通常需由领域和过程挖掘专家手动创建，成本高昂且耗时。这导致大量组织内部的非结构化知识（如事故工单、操作手册、文本报告）未被充分利用，限制了过程挖掘的潜力。

Method: 1. 构建了一个新的“文本到日志”数据集。
2. 提出一个可扩展框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）作为数据翻译器，连接非结构化文本与结构化事件数据。
3. 在该数据集上对LLMs进行微调，以增强其从非结构化资源中提取事件日志的能力。
4. 对比评估了微调方法与少样本（few-shot）及零样本（zero-shot）提示技术的性能。

Result: 1. 微调后的LLM能够从非结构化资源中提取高保真的事件日志。
2. 微调方法的性能显著优于少样本或零样本提示方法。
3. 研究证实，微调是生成可靠事件数据的必要前提条件。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个有前景的流程，使之前未使用的数据能够被引入过程挖掘生态系统，从而有效地扩展了利用过程挖掘进一步调查组织工作流的可能性。

Abstract: Process mining (PM) provides a powerful framework for discovering and optimizing operational processes from event data. However, the efficacy of PM techniques is strictly predicated on the availability of structured event logs. Thus far, event logs have often been laboriously created by domain and process mining experts. This costly effort causes large portions of organizational knowledge, including incident tickets, manuals, and textual reports, to remain underutilized. We address this bottleneck by investigating the efficacy of Large Language Models (LLMs) as automated data translators. We propose a scalable framework that leverages LLMs as data translators to bridge the gap between unstructured textual resources and structured event data. We finetune LLMs on a newly created text-to-log dataset, demonstrating that the resulting models can extract high-fidelity event logs from unstructured resources. Our results show that this finetuning approach outperforms few-shot or zero-shot prompting by a large amount, highlighting finetuning as a necessary pre-condition for generating reliable event data. We conclude that our method provides a promising pipeline for making previously unused data available to process mining ecosystems, effectively expanding the possibilities of using PM to further investigate organizational workflows.

</details>


### [361] [LEAP: Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for LLM-based Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01337)
*Yufei Chen,Yiran Zhao,Xiaogang Xu,Qipeng Xie,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEAP框架，通过分离证据评估与概率聚合，解决LLM预测中“单体预测”导致的证据影响模糊和不确定性坍缩问题，显著提升预测准确性与校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的预测系统多采用“单体预测”（Monolithic Prediction）设计，即让LLM一次性阅读所有证据并生成最终预测。这种设计存在两个主要缺陷：一是掩盖了单个证据对项目结果的具体影响；二是导致竞争结果间的不确定性信息丢失或坍缩。

Method: 提出LEAP（Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for Probabilistic forecasting）框架：1. 单独审视每项收集到的证据；2. 提取描述该证据对目标影响的似然参数；3. 结合显式先验和确定性概率模型，将这些似然值聚合成后验分布。该方法支持连续、单选和多选预测，并保证证据贡献的可复现性。

Result: 在涵盖预测、信息检索和浏览任务的基准测试中，LEAP在多种模型和代理CLI框架上均表现出优势。在控制先验访问、推理预算和聚合方式的情况下，LEAP在大多数预测准确性和校准指标上优于对比方法。

Conclusion: LEAP通过重构证据使用流程，实现了更透明、可复现且校准良好的概率预测，为LLM驱动的决策系统提供了更稳健的方法论基础。

Abstract: LLM-based forecasting systems have improved on real-world tasks such as financial markets and sports outcomes, largely through stronger search and tool use. Many systems still ask an LLM to read all collected evidence together and produce the final forecast. We call this design Monolithic Prediction. It can obscure how individual evidence items affect the result and collapse uncertainty across competing outcomes. We propose LEAP (Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for Probabilistic forecasting), which reorganizes how collected evidence is used in the prediction stage. LEAP examines each evidence item separately and elicits likelihood parameters that describe its implications for the target. An explicit prior and a deterministic probabilistic model then combine these likelihoods into a posterior distribution. This procedure supports continuous, single-choice, and multi-choice forecasts while preserving reproducible evidence contributions. We build a benchmark covering forecasting, information-seeking, and browsing tasks, and evaluate LEAP on our own agent loop and several agent CLI frameworks. Given the same evidence, LEAP improves most prediction and calibration metrics across models and remains stronger under controlled comparisons of prior access, inference budget, and aggregation.

</details>


### [362] [Cheap Verifiers, Large Blind Spots: Measuring the Reliability Cost of Cost-Saving Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01345)
*Dushyant Rajput*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了“自改进”推理级联系统的致命缺陷：虽然通过微调廉价模型可降低升级率，但验证器存在严重盲区，导致系统指标虚假繁荣而真实错误率飙升。结论是依赖自身验证器的指标无法评估此类系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索并评估将推理级联（Inference Cascades）与自我微调循环结合的效果，即利用前沿模型作为验证器，指导廉价学生模型进行纠错，以期降低计算成本和升级频率。

Method: 在真实大型语言模型上测量闭环级联系统；分析验证器对学生错误答案的接受率（盲区）；测试基于验证器拒绝样本的朴素纠正性微调效果；构建理论模型（双群体守恒定律）解释系统盲点，并通过合成研究验证机制。

Result: 1. 验证器存在巨大且对抗性的盲区（$\beta$随学生能力增强而增大，最坏情况出现在廉价组合中）；2. 使用前沿验证器虽能减小盲区，但会导致高达46%的升级率，抵消成本优势；3. 朴素微调不仅无效，反而导致小模型性能退化甚至崩溃；4. 系统内部指标显示恒定低错误率（3%），但真实错误率高达32%，系统对自身退化完全盲目。

Conclusion: 自改进级联系统的可靠性不能通过其自身验证器计算的任何指标来读取。由于内在的理论限制（双群体守恒定律），系统可能在所有内部指标改善的同时，实际质量却在下降，因此该类自我改进循环在当前规模下是自相矛盾的。

Abstract: Inference cascades cut cost by answering most queries with a cheap model and escalating a hard tail to a frontier model that acts as verifier. A natural extension closes the loop: fine-tune the cheap student on the verifier's rejections so the escalation rate, and cost, fall each round. We measure this loop on real LLMs and report four findings. First, the verifier's blind spot, the fraction of the student's wrong answers it accepts, is large and moves adversarially: it grows with student capability ($β$ from 0.12 to 0.55 as the student scales 0.5B to 32B) and shrinks with verifier capability, so it is worst in the cheap-student, cheap-verifier regime cascades exist to create. Second, buying it away returns the saving: a frontier verifier drives $β$ to about 0.05 but then escalates on 46% of hard-MATH queries against a 39% true error rate, paying the frontier price on nearly half of all traffic. Third, naive corrective fine-tuning on the verifier-rejected tail does not improve the small student but degrades and ultimately collapses it, across every teacher we tried (cross-family and same-family), so at this scale the self-improving loop is self-defeating. Fourth, through all of this the cascade's own dashboard, every metric computed through the verifier, reads a flat 3% error while true delivered error swings up to 32%: the system is blind to its own degradation by construction. We then give the theory that explains the blindness, a two-population conservation law, $ε_\infty \lesssim q_0 β_0$, under which every in-loop metric improves while true quality does not, and a synthetic study that validates the mechanism. The practical conclusion: the reliability of a self-improving cascade cannot be read from any metric computed through its own verifier.

</details>


### [363] [SymFold: Synergizing Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Accurate Protein Inverse Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01353)
*Handong Wang,Jiaxin Qi,Baisheng Lai,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种对称双路径架构，结合蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的序列进化知识和多模态蛋白质语言模型（MPLM）的结构知识，通过迭代引导实现更优的蛋白质逆折叠性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常遵循串行流程，依赖结构编码器生成粗略序列后由PLM进行后期精炼，但受限于上游预测质量；而直接利用MPLM进行逆折叠的效果不佳。旨在解决单一模态或串行处理带来的局限性，提升逆折叠准确性。

Method: 引入对称双路径架构，同时利用PLM获取预训练的序列进化知识，并利用MPLM获取预训练的结构知识，两者协同迭代地指导蛋白质序列生成。

Result: 在标准的蛋白质逆折叠基准测试中达到最先进水平（SOTA），超越现有方法；消融实验验证了对称设计的合理性。

Conclusion: 该对称双路径设计为蛋白质逆折叠任务提供了有效的新方向，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Protein inverse folding aims to recover amino acid sequences for a given 3D protein structure, underpinning broad applications such as enzyme engineering and drug discovery.Current methods often follow a serial pipeline, in which a structure encoder predicts a coarse sequence, which is then refined by protein language models (PLMs). However, because PLMs only perform post-hoc sequence edits, the refinement is bounded by the quality of upstream predictions.Thanks to recent multimodal protein language models (MPLMs), we could directly encode structure to generate sequences with pretrained structural knowledge, but we observe that they are not effective for inverse folding. Therefore, we introduce a symmetric dual-path architecture that both leverages PLMs for pretrained sequence evolution knowledge and MPLMs for pretrained structural knowledge to iteratively guide protein sequence generation.Through extensive experiments across standard protein inverse folding benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior approaches, and ablation studies validate the rationale of our symmetric design, revealing a promising direction for the community.

</details>


### [364] [EDGE: Error Dependency Graph-Guided Multi-Error Attribution in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01360)
*Jun Hou,Priya Pitre,Yi Fang,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EDGE框架通过构建错误依赖图并验证因果子集，解决了LLM代理失败中多错误归因的问题，显著提升了多错误类别的归因准确性和解释可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法通常只识别单一责任主体、步骤或根本原因，未能显式建模错误之间的依赖关系，而LLM代理失败往往包含多个相互关联的错误。

Method: 提出EDGE（Error Dependency Graph-guided multi-Error attribution framework），从观测到的错误事件中构建错误依赖图，并通过反事实 rollout 验证可靠的因果子集；利用推理图指导两阶段“LLM-as-judge”检测器进行错误归因，并利用干预验证的子图提供解释和修复分析的基础。

Result: 在TRAIL和MAST数据集上的实验表明，EDGE在大多数评估模型和设置下提高了类别级多错误归因性能；在使用Who&When风格提示词的实验中，该图结构在不同提示策略下均表现出辅助作用。

Conclusion: 错误依赖结构是优于孤立根因预测的有效诊断先验，为理解代理失败提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agent failures often contain multiple related errors rather than a single mistake. Existing attribution methods usually identify a responsible agent, step, or root cause, but do not explicitly model dependency between errors. We introduce EDGE, an Error Dependency Graph-guided multi-Error attribution framework. EDGE constructs an error dependency graph from observed error events and validates a reliable causal subset through counterfactual rollout. The inference graph guides a two-stage LLM-as-judge detector for error attribution, and the intervention-validated subgraph provides a more reliable basis for explanation and repair analysis. Experiments on TRAIL and MAST show that EDGE improves category-level multi-error attribution across most evaluated models and settings. Experiments with adapted Who&When-style prompts show that the graph helps across prompting strategies. These results suggest that dependency structure is a useful diagnostic prior for agent failures beyond isolated root-cause prediction.

</details>


### [365] [A survey of AI-generated voices and their detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15411)
*Chengzhe Sun,Tianle Yang,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI语音生成与检测技术，针对语音克隆带来的欺诈和虚假信息风险，分析了其检测难点（相比图像/视频更深奥的音韵学挑战），并梳理了最新进展、基准资源及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成逼真人声技术的快速进步，虽然推动了无障碍工具和虚拟助手的发展，但也导致了冒充、欺诈和虚假信息等有害用途。近期针对企业和政治领袖的语音克隆诈骗事件凸显了建立强大安全措施的紧迫性。

Method: 提供AI语音生成和检测方法的全面概览，涵盖技术基础和最先进的最新进展，同时识别关键开放性问题、基准资源和未来方向。

Result: 揭示了合成语音检测的独特挑战，包括音韵学、韵律和听觉感知的复杂性；整理了当前的技术状态、可用基准资源以及该领域未来的研究路径。

Conclusion: 该调查为研究人员提供了关于AI语音生成与检测的全面参考，旨在通过明确开放挑战和未来方向来促进该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: The ability of artificial intelligence (AI) models to generate highly realistic human voices has advanced rapidly. These technologies power accessibility tools, virtual assistants and creative applications, but they also enable harmful uses, including impersonation, fraud and disinformation. Recent incidents of voice cloning scams targeting businesses and political leaders underscore the urgent need for robust safeguards. Unlike image and video deepfakes, the detection of synthetic voices poses unique challenges due to the complexity of phonetics, prosody and auditory perception. This survey offers a comprehensive overview of AI voice generation and detection methods, encompassing both the technical foundations and the latest state-of-the-art advances. This study also identifies key open challenges, benchmark resources and future directions to make this survey useful for future researchers.

</details>


### [366] [Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction (NeuSOGA): From Observations to Symbolic Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01408)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NeuSOGA框架，将几何观测转化为可解释、可编辑的显式符号数学表示，解决AI中感知知识与推理脱节的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过大规模统计学习获得强大感知能力，但知识编码于难以检查和操纵的潜在参数中。旨在借鉴神经符号AI和人类抽象理论，实现从观测到显式符号表示的转化，以支持抽象、解释和推理。

Method: 提出NeuSOGA（神经符号几何抽象）框架，结合欧几里得距离变换进行拓扑引导的结构发现、Segment Anything基础模型感知、自适应多尺度几何抽象以及隐式样条符号合成，逐步将观测转化为拓扑、几何及最终的符号数学表示。

Result: 生成的表示为解析隐式模型，支持任意阶平滑性、加法组合和闭式评估。在ModelNet40点云、任意视角投影和分割光学观测实验表明，该框架能跨传感模态和视角保留关键的几何与拓扑结构，生成紧凑且可解释的符号表示。

Conclusion: NeuSOGA提供了从观测到符号的可解释和可解释路径，建立了连接感知与符号推理的桥梁。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence is the transformation of observations into explicit symbolic representations suitable for abstraction, interpretation, and reasoning. While modern AI systems achieve remarkable perceptual capabilities through large-scale statistical learning, the resulting knowledge is typically encoded within latent parameters that are difficult to inspect or manipulate analytically. Inspired by Neuro-Symbolic AI and theories of human abstraction, this paper investigates the formation of symbolic mathematical representations from geometric observations.
  We propose NeuSOGA (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction), a framework that progressively transforms observations into topological abstractions, geometric abstractions, and ultimately symbolic mathematical representations. The architecture combines topology-guided structural discovery using Euclidean Distance Transforms, foundation-model perception using Segment Anything, adaptive multi-scale geometric abstraction, and symbolic synthesis through Implicit Area Splines.
  The resulting representation is an analytical implicit model supporting arbitrary-order smoothness, additive composition, and closed-form evaluation. Unlike neural latent encodings, the generated representation remains interpretable, editable, and mathematically explicit. Experiments on ModelNet40 point clouds, arbitrary-view projections, and segmented optical observations demonstrate that NeuSOGA transforms diverse observations into compact symbolic representations while preserving essential geometric and topological structure across sensing modalities and viewing directions.
  NeuSOGA provides an interpretable and explainable pathway from observation to symbol and establishes

</details>


### [367] [EdiTikZ: Scientific Figure Editing from Revision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01409)
*Christian Greisinger,Zhixue Zhao,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DaEdiTikZ数据集和模型，利用自然科学修订轨迹训练VLM进行科学图表编辑，在自动和人工评估中超越GPT-5.6-Sol。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学图表生成需迭代优化，但编辑任务缺乏有效监督数据；现有方法依赖昂贵专有系统或合成数据，难以满足出版级需求。

Method: 从arXiv、GitHub等挖掘391K个TikZ编辑对，构建DaEdiTikZ数据集；训练Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ模型（4B/9B），联合学习重建与编辑，并通过强化学习优化渲染保真度和编辑应用。

Result: 9B模型在自动评估中优于所有基线，在790实例的人工基准测试中表现优于GPT-5.6-Sol，接近Gemini-3.1-Pro水平，且在分布外偏移下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DaEdiTikZ提供可扩展的自然编辑监督源，EdiTikZ模型显著提升科学图表编辑能力，为后续研究和应用开源了模型与数据集。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in generating scientific figures from text or images. However, producing publication-ready figures requires iterative refinement, making scientific figure editing an important yet largely unexplored task. Existing approaches rely on costly proprietary agentic systems, focus primarily on evaluation, or construct training supervision from synthetically generated edits. Instead, we leverage naturally occurring scientific revision and development trajectories as a scalable source of supervision. To this end, we introduce DaEdiTikZ, the first large-scale dataset of revision-derived scientific figure edits, constructed by mining 391K plausible TikZ edit pairs from arXiv, GitHub, and TeX SE and inferring 781K directed edit instructions with a VLM conditioned on rendered figures and TikZ code. We further introduce DaEdiTikZ-Bench, a human-refined benchmark with 790 instances, and train two compact Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ models (4B and 9B) by jointly learning reconstruction and editing, followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with complementary rewards for rendered fidelity and edit application. Automatic evaluation places our 9B model above all tested baselines, while human evaluation with 9 annotators and 4,320 ratings places it above GPT-5.6-Sol and on par with Gemini-3.1-Pro. Under severe out-of-distribution shifts, it remains competitive with GPT-5.6-Sol near its 2K training sequence-length regime. Models and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [368] [Parsing the Stream: A Live Trace Model for Long-Horizon Agents and Their Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01466)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种“实时轨迹模型”（live trace model），通过将长周期智能体的原始事件日志增量折叠为类型化的运行状态，解决了人类观察者和智能体自身因上下文限制无法处理过长轨迹的问题。该方法在降低token消耗和成本的同时，显著提高了监控准确性和任务执行成功率，并提供了确定性的可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期智能体的运行轨迹（trace）过于庞大，超出了两个主要消费者的处理能力：一是监测运行过程的人类观察者，二是受限于有界上下文的智能体自身。需要将原始轨迹折叠回适合消费者处理的格式。

Method: 构建了一个追加式事件账本（append-only event ledger），将其增量折叠为类型化的运行状态，并为不同消费者编译生成特定的视图。通过确定性基准真相对两种消费者场景进行评估：使用LLM作为人类观察者的代理进行监控问答评估；在120链接的序列依赖任务中评估智能体的执行能力。

Result: 1. **观察者侧**：相比直接读取原始轨迹，编译后的视图使输入token减少约14-15倍，成本降低5-7倍，且准确率显著提高（0.85-0.87 vs 0.48）。
2. **智能体侧**：在序列依赖任务中，维护每步统计信息的机制成功率为30/30，而全上下文提示仅为8/30。折叠方法的准确性与提示级草稿纸相当，但成本更低。
3. **价值分析**：折叠方法的核心优势在于其确定性聚合带来的准确性、紧凑性带来的成本优势，以及比廉价替代方案更具优势的确定性可审计性和统一的状态服务。

Conclusion: 基于观察到的失败案例推导出了十一个轨迹折叠候选要求，并通过顺序敏感任务族划定了该方法的适用边界。研究释放了代码、基准测试、可再生合成语料库及所有工作bench轨迹数据，推动了相关领域的开放研究。

Abstract: A long-horizon agent's trace outgrows both of its consumers: the human observer monitoring the run, and the agent itself, whose bounded context the trace must be folded back into. We present a live trace model, an append-only event ledger folded incrementally into typed run state and compiled into per-consumer views, and evaluate it for both consumers against deterministic ground truth. For the observer side, evaluated with an LLM reader as proxy, the compiled view answers monitoring questions using approximately 14x and 15x fewer input tokens (by reader) and at 5-7x lower cost than a budget-capped single-call reading of the raw trace, with higher accuracy (0.85-0.87 versus 0.48). Because the questions were co-designed with the view schema, we treat the token and cost reduction, conditional on schema coverage, as the transferable result. For the agent, on 120-link sequential-dependency tasks, mechanisms that maintain the task's running statistic in per-step state succeed where full-context prompting fails (30/30 versus 8/30 under a clean protocol, n=30, labeled descriptive owing to benchmark-system co-development); a prompt-level scratchpad matches the fold's accuracy at lower cost, and a two-arm decomposition attributes the fold's accuracy to its deterministic aggregate and its cost advantage to its compactness. The fold's remaining value over cheaper alternatives is deterministic auditability and serving the observer from the same state. We derive eleven candidate requirements for trace folding from observed failures and delimit them with an order-sensitive task family on which the fold ceases to help. Code, benchmarks, a regenerable synthetic corpus, and all workbench traces are released.

</details>


### [369] [Harness-of-Harness: Multi-Day Autonomous Software Development with Continual Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01481)
*Haoyang Yan,Min-le Su,Hangfan Zhang,Zhanhao Li,Chen Zhang,Shao Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Harness-of-Harness (HoH)框架，通过迭代规划-编码-测试循环和平衡修复与能力增长，使LLM编码代理在无人工干预下持续改进软件。在多个基准测试中平均提升52.25%，并成功自主开发完整的第一人称射击游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM基于代码代理在自主软件开发中难以持续改进软件质量的问题，旨在实现从高层需求到完整、可用软件系统的无干预转化。

Method: 构建HoH框架，利用现有编码代理工具包，组织成迭代循环。核心机制包括：平衡修复与能力成长、将开发分解为可验证的小增量、分离实施时测试与独立评估、约束可验证输出而非规定工作流、逐步暴露交付物/工具/技能、鼓励复用、维护版本历史。

Result: 在GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE和ProgramBench上，三种“工具包-模型”组合（Codex+GPT-5.5, OpenCode+DeepSeek-V4-Pro, Pi+MiniMax-M3）均优于独立工具包，三次迭代后平均相对增益52.25%，最高82.86%。多日部署70+次迭代后，自主开发出包含剧情、核心机制、可玩性、视听效果的FPS游戏。

Conclusion: HoH框架能有效支持LLM编码代理的自主软件开发，通过结构化迭代和策略优化显著提升软件功能和质量，证明了其在复杂软件生成任务中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: This paper studies autonomous software development, in which LLM-based coding agents transform high-level requirements into complete, functional, and usable software systems without human intervention. We introduce Harness-of-Harness (HoH), a framework that enables coding agents to continually improve software during autonomous development. HoH operates on existing coding-agent harnesses, and organizes their executions into iterative planning-coding-testing loops. To sustain improvement across loops, HoH balances repair with capability growth, scopes development into small and verifiable increments, separates implementation-time testing from independent evaluation, and constrains verifiable outputs rather than prescribing agent workflows. It progressively exposes deliverables, role-specific tools, and skills, encourages reuse rather than recreation, and maintains versioned project histories. On GameCraft-Bench, FrontierSWE, and ProgramBench, three harness-model pairs (Codex with GPT-5.5, OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Pi with MiniMax-M3), HoH consistently outperforms the corresponding standalone harnesses, achieving an average relative gain of 52.25 percent and a maximum gain of 82.86 percent after three iterations. In a multi-day deployment with more than 70 iterations, HoH autonomously develops a first-person-shooter game, featuring a coherent storyline, fully implemented core mechanics, human-playable experience, polished visuals and integrated audio. Github: https://github.com/Flesymeb/HarnessOfHarness Project Page: https://flesymeb.github.io/HarnessOfHarness/

</details>


### [370] [When Guardrails Look Effective: Construct Validity Failures in LLM Agent Commerce Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01519)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了基于语言模型代理的市场交互模拟中的效度风险，发现原始报告的福利增益可能由协议隔离和随机性导致而非政策本身。通过构建效度合同，指出在通过激励有效性、协议隔离和随机稳定性检查前，任何实质性政策主张均为无效或不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着交互式模拟越来越多地用于评估由语言模型代理组成的市场中的政策，其输出（如价格、利润、消费者剩余）看似具有经济学意义，但未必真实反映所声称的行为机制。因此需要审计这种“虚假经济表现”的风险，确保模拟结果的构念效度。

Method: 构建一个多轮买家-卖家测试床用于可配置酒店预订交易。对初始实现进行审计，控制模式（schema）和买家选择者，对比受保护与未受保护代理的差异。分析不同生成次数下的效应变化及残差变异。引入脚本化阳性对照（profit-maximizing seller）检验激励结构。提出包含激励有效性、协议隔离、随机稳定性和福利核算的构念效度合同。

Result: 1. 初始报告显示护栏（guardrails）带来显著福利增益（+87.4等），但在固定模式和选择者后，差异大幅缩小甚至出现负值（-13.9）。2. 14B模型单次生成平均效应为+229，三次生成后降至+37.6且置信区间跨越零，表明结果不稳定。3. 生成残差解释了49.9%的变异。4. 卖方激励检查呈非单调性：增加利润压力反而降低利润。5. 脚本化对照显示，最优利润卖方已实现第一优福利，护栏主要重新分配并减少福利，仅在强制低效捆绑时创造福利。6. 原始估计在协议隔离下被判定为INVALID，受控研究在激励有效性和随机稳定性下为INCONCLUSIVE。

Conclusion: 模拟中观察到的护栏价值可能是未被识别的伪影，直到模拟代理和协议通过严格的效度检查。研究贡献了一个构念效度合同框架，要求在得出实质性政策结论前，必须验证激励有效性、协议隔离性和随机稳定性，否则应返回INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE。这强调了在LLM代理模拟中进行严格方法论审计的重要性。

Abstract: Interactive simulations increasingly evaluate policies in markets populated by language-model agents. Their outputs can look economic---prices, profits, consumer surplus, and welfare---without instantiating the behavior named in the claim. We audit this risk in a multi-turn buyer--seller testbed for configurable hotel transactions. An initial implementation reported welfare gains from two marketplace guardrails of +87.4, +35.0, and +28.8 across a Qwen2.5 1.5B--14B ladder. It also gave guarded and unguarded agents different offer schemas and choice procedures. Holding the schema and buyer chooser fixed changes the paired contrasts to +7.2, -13.9, and +23.8. The four largest 14B single-generation effects averaged +229; after three generations per profile-condition, they averaged +37.6 (95% bootstrap interval [-34.2, 109.3]), while generation residuals account for 49.9% of variation in this post-hoc probe. A seller-incentive check is non-monotone: increasing profit pressure produces less profit than the default seller prompt. Scripted positive controls show why this matters. A profit-maximizing seller already attains first-best welfare, so guardrails mostly redistribute and reduce welfare; they create welfare only when the seller is explicitly programmed to force inefficient bundles. We contribute a construct-validity contract separating incentive validity, protocol isolation, stochastic stability, and welfare accounting, and returning INVALID or INCONCLUSIVE before substantive policy claims. In our case, the original estimate is INVALID under protocol isolation, while the controlled study remains INCONCLUSIVE under incentive validity and stochastic stability. The case does not show that guardrails are ineffective; it shows their apparent value is unidentified until the simulated agents and protocol pass these checks.

</details>


### [371] [EvoSCM: Scientific Belief Revision Through Causal Model Evolution and Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01526)
*Qing Zhao,Haowei Li,Weijian Deng,Pengxu Wei,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoSCM框架，通过显式结构因果模型（SCM）和闭环发现机制，提升科学智能体在物理世界探索中的假设构建、实验设计与验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）智能体通常以自由文本形式表达科学假设，导致其信念隐含且难以测试或修正，限制了科学推理的可验证性。

Method: 引入EvoSCM，维护竞争性SCM假设种群；通过闭环发现循环：从证据中溯因潜在机制、设计判别性干预、做出可证伪预测并进行实验；根据预测与观察的差异归纳校正规则以修订因果结构，并演绎验证修订后的假设种群。

Result: 在DiscoverPhysics基准测试中，EvoSCM持续优于基线方法，提供更准确的解释和预测，并更有效地利用实验交互。

Conclusion: 显式结构化因果模型结合动态演化机制，能显著提升智能体在非规范物理世界中的科学发现能力和假设修正效率。

Abstract: Scientific agents must learn not only how to reason, but also what to believe. However, existing LLM agents typically express scientific hypotheses in free-form text, leaving their beliefs implicit and difficult to test or revise. We introduce EvoSCM, which equips scientific agents with explicit structural causal models that evolve as new experimental evidence is collected. EvoSCM maintains a population of competing SCM hypotheses, each encoding a candidate causal explanation of the environment, and evolves them through a closed discovery loop. In each round, the agent abduces latent mechanisms from accumulated evidence, designs discriminative interventions, and commits to falsifiable predictions that it tests through experimentation. Discrepancies between prediction and observation are inductively distilled into correction rules that revise the causal structures and mechanisms of each hypothesis, and the agent then deductively validates the revised population against accumulated evidence and structural consistency to guide the next round. We evaluate EvoSCM on DiscoverPhysics, a benchmark requiring agents to uncover the hidden dynamics of noncanonical physical worlds through experimentation. EvoSCM consistently improves scientific discovery over baselines, yielding more accurate explanations and predictions while making more effective use of experimental interactions.

</details>


### [372] [Can LLMs Discover Scientific Laws in Real and Parallel Worlds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01552)
*Yiming Huang,Ziche Liu,Zhuohang Wu,Yiqian Wang,Junxia Cui,Xinkai Zou,Linjun Mao,Nan Huang,Naicheng Yu,Kaijie Zhu,Yue Ma,Kun Zhou,Letian Peng,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCILAWS-BENCH，一个基于真实科学数据和已发表论文的科学定律发现基准，包含118个问题、291个候选定律和约800万个数据点。该基准通过两种设置（固定观测预测和主动查询合成定律）评估大语言模型在AI for Science中的能力，揭示了预测拟合度与科学有效性之间的差异及记忆效应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法要么简化为合成环境，要么重用可能已被大模型记忆的已发表目标，无法准确评估模型是否真正具备发现科学定律的能力及其评估标准。

Method: 构建SCILAWS-BENCH基准，涵盖6个学科领域。设计两种互补任务设置：(1) SCILAWS-REAL：基于固定真实观测数据提出定律，评估未见数据的预测拟合度和文献来源的科学有效性；(2) SCILAWS-PARALLEL：通过主动查询残差校准的世界，恢复从已发表形式合成的隐藏定律。

Result: 研究发现预测拟合度可能与科学有效性不一致；模型的记忆程度决定了其是复现还是超越已发表公式；最佳-of-N研究揭示了选择瓶颈问题。

Conclusion: 提供了一个以论文为基准的新评估工具，并为评估AI科学发现提供了新的实证视角，强调了在保留科学语境的同时区分固定记录发现和主动恢复新定律的重要性。

Abstract: Scientific equation discovery has long been central to scientific progress, proceeding through iterative cycles of hypothesis generation, observational testing, and refinement under scientific constraints. As LLM capabilities advance and their role in AI for Science expands, it remains an open problem whether they can genuinely discover scientific laws and how this ability should be evaluated. Existing evaluations, however, often either simplify discovery through synthetic settings or reuse published targets that may already be familiar to LLMs. We therefore introduce SCILAWS-BENCH, a benchmark for scientific law discovery built from published research and real scientific data. It comprises 118 problems drawn from 381 scientific papers, covering 291 candidate laws and roughly 8M real data points across six scientific disciplines. Each problem is instantiated in two complementary settings: (1) SCILAWS-REAL asks models to propose laws from fixed real observations and evaluates held-out predictive fit and scientific validity derived from the source literature, and (2) SCILAWS-PARALLEL asks models to actively query residual-calibrated worlds and recover synthesized hidden laws derived from published forms. This two-setting task design preserves each problem's scientific context while separately evaluating fixed-record law discovery and active recovery of a newly synthesized hidden law. We find that predictive fit can diverge from scientific validity, memorization shapes whether models reproduce or move beyond published formulas, and our best-of-N study reveals a selection bottleneck. Our work provides a paper-grounded benchmark and new empirical perspectives for evaluating AI for scientific discovery. Project page: https://yiyihum.github.io/SciLaws-Bench

</details>


### [373] [Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy: Learning Autonomous Policies from Imperfect VLM Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01567)
*Matteo Merler,Giovanni Bonetta,Davide Zago,Rossella Cancelliere,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过选择性查询VLM（仅在智能体不确定时）并将指导蒸馏为轻量级RL策略，解决直接使用VLM作为策略成本高、僵化的问题。该方法在评估时无需VLM参与，且能超越VLM教师表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉-语言模型(VLMs)为交互决策提供了有用先验，但直接将其用作策略存在成本高（需每步查询）、缺乏环境交互学习能力以及容易重复系统性错误等缺陷。

Method: 提出SAGE（Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy）框架：1. 仅在学习者不确定时查询昂贵的VLM教师；2. 在训练期间执行建议动作；3. 将指导蒸馏至轻量级强化学习(RL)策略；4. 利用环境衍生的优势而非固定权重来加权教师动作蒸馏，以应对VLM建议的不可靠性。

Result: 在稀疏奖励视觉推理和导航任务中，SAGE学到的策略在评估时无需VLM指导即可运行，并在多个环境中优于无引导的RL，部分场景下甚至超越了VLM教师。结果证实，当VLM能帮助发现高奖励轨迹时选择性指导最有效；同时显著减少了VLM调用次数，部署阶段零调用。

Conclusion: VLMs无需作为固定策略使用即可发挥作用；它们可作为临时的、不完美的指导源，其价值通过与环境的交互进行测试并被内部化，从而构建更高效、自主的智能体。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide useful priors for interactive decision-making, but using them directly as policies is expensive and brittle: they must be queried at every step, do not improve from environment interaction, and can repeat systematic errors. We study how to learn a cheap autonomous policy from an online, expensive, and imperfect but informative VLM teacher. We propose SAGE (Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy), a framework that queries a VLM only when the learner is uncertain, executes the suggested action during training, and distills guidance into a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy. Because VLM advice is not always reliable, SAGE can weight teacher-action distillation using environment-derived advantages rather than treating all suggestions as equally useful. Across sparse-reward visual reasoning and navigation tasks, SAGE learns policies that act without VLM guidance at evaluation time and improves over unguided RL in several environments, including settings where the learned policy exceeds its VLM teacher. The results show that selective guidance is most beneficial when the VLM can help the agent discover high-reward trajectories, and less useful when unguided exploration already succeeds or teacher actions do not lead to informative experience. SAGE also reduces VLM usage by prompting the teacher only on a fraction of training steps and requiring no VLM calls at deployment. Overall, our results suggest that VLMs don't need to be used as fixed policies to be useful; they can instead act as temporary, imperfect sources of guidance whose value is tested and internalized through interaction.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [374] [SilentProbe: Measuring Silent Failure in Production APIs Used as Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00035)
*Zongrong Li,Shengkun Ye,Feiyou Guo,Zuoyou Dang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LLM代理常因API文档约束描述不清（仅文本无机器可读约束）而遭遇“静默失败”。研究发现，将词汇表明确写入Schema可彻底解决此问题，这比优化模型本身更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理调用生产环境API时，无法区分“查询无匹配结果”与“服务器未理解请求”，两者均返回HTTP 200且无异常抛出，导致代理难以进行错误处理或分支判断。

Method: 1. 审计2,501个OpenAPI文档中的参数约束声明情况；2. 通过Monid聚合层对27家供应商的活跃商业端点执行219种基于Schema的扰动测试；3. 评估8大系列共12个LLM模型在普通任务中的表现及故障恢复能力；4. 对比Schema中显式定义词汇表与仅文本描述的效能差异。

Result: 1. 仅7.5%的文档声明了枚举类型，15.2%包含机器可检查约束，40.1%仅有文本描述；2. 机器可检查约束能产生诚实的错误报告（111/111），而纯文本约束静默失败率极高（44/61）；3. 模型在仅文本描述的词汇上识别率为0%，而在完整定义的词汇上正确率达88-91%；4. 代理检测出12%的静默失败，但修复率为0%，41%时间给出虚假否定，12%时间捏造数据；5. 将词汇纳入Schema后，失败率从88/88降至0/89。

Conclusion: API Schema的机器可读性（特别是显式定义约束和枚举）是预测API响应诚实性的关键因素。通过在Schema中添加一行代码来明确约束，比提升LLM模型性能更能有效消除静默失败问题。

Abstract: An LLM agent calling a production API cannot distinguish a query that matched nothing from a query the server did not understand. Both return HTTP 200 with a parsable body, no exception to catch and no field to branch on. We ask what predicts which one occurred, and what it does to the agent. Auditing 721,320 parameters across 2,501 independently published OpenAPI documents, we find that 7.5% declare an enumeration and 15.2% declare any machine-checkable constraint at all, while 40.1% of documents state at least one constraint in prose that their schema does not encode. Executing 219 schema-derived perturbations against live commercial endpoints from 27 vendors, reached through a single aggregation layer (Monid) that publishes a schema and returns a run identifier for every call, we find that constraint form, not vendor identity, predicts honesty: machine-checkable constraints yielded an honest error in 111 of 111 cases, prose-only constraints failed silently in 44 of 61 (p = 2e-13). Twelve models across eight families then met these endpoints on ordinary tasks. A vocabulary that the description merely exemplifies was missed by every model on 88 of 88 attempts, while vocabularies written out in full were used correctly 88 to 91% of the time. Running the full agent loop, models detected the resulting silent failure in 12% of cases, repaired it in 0%, asserted a false negative to the user in 41%, and invented a figure in 12%. Promoting the vocabulary into the schema removes the failure, from 88 of 88 to 0 of 89. The fix is one line of schema rather than a better model. Code, schemas, perturbation sets, agent transcripts and per-call run identifiers are released at https://github.com/Jasper0122/silentprobe.

</details>


### [375] [Two-Sided State-Space Models for Sequential Recommendation with Non-Random Multimodal Review Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00165)
*Ziwen Pan,Zihan Liang,Ruoxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TS-SSM模型，通过非随机评论融合、用户状态动态演化及物品状态不对称反馈机制，解决双边数字平台中序列推荐的两大盲区，显著提升推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐系统将评论视为被动信号，忽略了评论生成的非随机性（依赖用户和物品的潜在状态）以及评论对物品状态重塑和用户决策的反向影响。

Method: 构建双向状态空间模型（TS-SSM），包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于缺失非随机假设的多模态融合模块，编码评论内容与观测模式；(2) 结合时间变异与局部图消息传递的用户状态演化机制；(3) 具有正负评论反馈非对称继承特性的物品状态演化机制。

Result: 在六个Amazon类别上，Recall@20比BSARec提升14.8%-18.8%，平均超过HM4SR 11.7%；在Goodreads Fantasy数据集上，Recall@20较HM4SR从0.5191提升至0.5847。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: TS-SSM有效捕捉了评论生成与消费的动态交互过程，通过显式建模用户偏好更新与物品状态演化的双向影响，显著增强了事件条件化序列推荐的性能。

Abstract: Two-sided digital platforms are inherently dynamic: user preferences shift, item popularity evolves, and reviews both reflect and drive these changes. Yet most sequential recommendation systems treat reviews as passive signals for updating user states, leaving two aspects underexplored. First, review generation is nonrandom, depending on evolving latent states of both users and items. Second, reviews can reshape item states, induce spillover across related items, and influence future user decisions. To address these gaps, we propose a two-sided state-space model (TS-SSM) for event-conditioned sequential recommendation. TS-SSM consists of three components: (1) a modality-missing-not-at-random fusion module that encodes review content and informative observation patterns; (2) user-state evolution with temporal variation and local graph message passing that uses related item states to refine user preferences; and (3) item-state evolution with asymmetric carryover of positive and negative review feedback. In experiments across six Amazon categories, TS-SSM increases Recall@20 over BSARec by 14.8%--18.8% and exceeds HM4SR by 11.7% on average. On Goodreads Fantasy, Recall@20 improves HM4SR from .5191 to .5847. Ablations highlight distinct contributions of observation patterns, local propagation, and item dynamics.

</details>


### [376] [MUSES: A Benchmark for Prospective Intellectual-Roots Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00313)
*Rohan Pandey,Sunjae Kwon,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MUSES基准和CiteRoots数据集，用于评估基于未来影响力而非当前流行度的“前瞻性智力根源”检索。实验表明，现有方法在识别较少见但具有启发性的论文时性能显著下降，揭示了当前检索系统对中心论文的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文献检索系统主要优化相关性和流行度，倾向于推荐核心论文，而忽视了那些虽不常见但后续被证明具有高度生成性（generative）的文献。科学发现依赖于找到塑造后续研究的早期文献，因此需要一种能识别这些“前瞻性智力根源”的新评估标准。

Method: 1. **MUSES基准**：构建了一个包含233万篇论文的大规模基准，拥有约14万个测试实例，按熟悉度分层。这是首个在该规模下具备共享检索任务和作者确认标签的前瞻性基准。
2. **CiteRoots数据集**：包含两个层级：
   - 可扩展修辞层：基于局部引用文本，使用LLM作为裁判（与人类黄金标准的一致性κ=0.896）。
   - 作者认可层：来自753篇焦点论文的1,518对生成性-灵感配对数据。
3. **难度轴**：沿两个维度组织难度：熟悉度轴（CiteNext, CiteNew, CiteNew-Isolated）和功能轴（广泛引用、修辞根源、作者认可根源）。
4. **模型评估**：在9类方法中测试，最强模型为基于SPECTER2的轻量级多质心检索器。

Result: 1. **性能衰减**：随着任务难度增加，Hit@100指标显著下降：从CiteNext的0.534降至CiteNew的0.424，修辞CiteNew的0.205，以及作者认可CiteNew的0.171，降幅达3.1倍。
2. **未解决问题比例高**：在注册的全测试审计中，即使K=1000，仍有约一半的广泛层级测试实例未被解决。
3. **概念差异性**：修辞角色与作者认可截然不同，同一裁判在这两者间的一致性极低（κ=0.037）。
4. **最佳模型**：基于SPECTER2的多质心检索器在所有方法类别中表现最强。

Conclusion: MUSES基准及其配套数据集CiteRoots揭示了当前检索系统在识别非主流但具启发性的“智力根源”方面的局限性。研究释放了MUSES、CiteRoots的两个层级以及一个蒸馏后的开放辅助裁判工具，旨在推动未来关于前瞻性检索和科学发现脉络的研究。

Abstract: Scientific discovery depends on finding prior literature that shapes what comes next. Existing retrieval systems optimize for relevance and popularity, often favoring central papers over less familiar works that later prove generative. We introduce \textbf{MUSES}, a million-instance benchmark for prospective intellectual-roots retrieval over a fixed 2.33M-paper corpus, with roughly 140K test instances per familiarity tier. To our knowledge, it is the first prospective benchmark at this scale with a shared retrieval task and author-confirmed paper-level root labels. Alongside it, \textbf{CiteRoots} pairs a scalable rhetorical layer over local citation text (LLM judge $κ= 0.896$ versus human gold) with a paper-level author-endorsed layer ($n = 1{,}518$ generative-inspiration pairs from 753 focal papers). MUSES organizes difficulty along two axes: a \emph{familiarity} axis spanning CiteNext, CiteNew, and CiteNew-Isolated, and a \emph{functional} axis spanning broad citations, rhetorical roots, and author-endorsed roots. Across 9 method classes, a lean multi-centroid retriever built on SPECTER2 is strongest. Hit@100 falls from 0.534 on CiteNext to 0.424 on CiteNew, 0.205 on rhetorical CiteNew, and 0.171 on author-endorsed CiteNew, a $3.1\times$ decline. In a registered eight-lens full-test audit, roughly half of broad-tier test instances remain unsolved at K=1{,}000. Rhetorical role and author endorsement are distinct: the same judge agrees with endorsement at $κ= 0.037$. We release MUSES, both CiteRoots layers, and a distilled open companion judge for future work on prospective retrieval and intellectual roots.

</details>


### [377] [Closed Forms and Synthetic Twins: Predicting Approximate Nearest Neighbor Recall from Embedding Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00364)
*Shmuel Herman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需构建索引即可预测近似检索性能的方法，通过标签无关的嵌入统计量实现召回率预测，并引入可训练的几何目标以统一提升各类索引的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入模型训练和评估假设检索是精确的，但在生产环境中通常使用近似索引（如HNSW、IVF、PQ等）。这些索引的行为在基准测试中未被观测到，导致实际召回率远低于预期（例如某编码器仅恢复14%的精确Top-10结果）。传统的修正方法（如白化）需要针对语料库拟合，存储成本高且难以适应动态变化的语料库。

Method: 利用原始嵌入的标签无关统计量，针对不同索引家族构建预测工具：(1) 对固定网格量化器（PQ, FDE）使用闭式矩统计；(2) 对分区索引使用合成双胞胎语料库进行模拟；(3) 对图索引使用大小外推且轻微校准的双胞胎语料库。此外，通过优化“得分边际”（score margin这一无法通过事后变换移动的特征）来训练嵌入模型的几何结构。

Result: 在未见过的一百万文档语料库上，预测召回率与实测召回率的误差仅为0.03。该方法能够同时提升所有索引家族的召回率，且任务成本较低。实现了从单次廉价测量中预测索引选择、修正成本和产量召回率的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法消除了对每语料库转换机制的依赖，适合持续变化的语料库环境。通过将编码器适配于几何结构而非耦合到单一索引，推动了召回率-计算前沿的发展，为生产环境中的检索系统提供了更可靠、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Embedding models are trained and evaluated as if retrieval were exact; in production they serve behind approximate indexes -- HNSW, IVF, product quantization, or the fixed-dimensional encodings (FDEs) of late-interaction models -- whose behavior the encoder's benchmarks never see: one modern encoder recovers just 14% of its exact top-10 through its raw FDE index. Such failures surface only after an index is built, and the standard patches -- corpus-fitted transforms such as whitening -- must be fitted, stored, and refit as the corpus changes, and can silently rewrite what the encoder returns. This paper shows that index behavior is predictable before anything is built, from label-free statistics of the raw embeddings, through a ladder of instruments matched to what each index family consumes: (1) closed-form moment statistics for the fixed-grid quantizers (PQ, FDE); (2) simulation on a synthetic twin corpus -- cluster statistics made generative, on which any index, composed production systems included, can be built and tested -- for partition indexes; (3) size-extrapolated, lightly calibrated twins for graph indexes at million-document scale. Predictions land within 0.03 of measured recall on an unseen million-document corpus. The same geometry is trainable: targeting the one statistic no post-hoc transform can move -- the score margin -- lifts recall for every index family at once, at a small measured task cost. The result: index choice, correction pricing, and production recall forecast from one cheap measurement pass, on new corpora and new indexes alike; serving without per-corpus transform machinery, suited to continuously changing corpora; and a recall-compute frontier pushed by adapting encoders to geometry rather than coupling them to any single index.

</details>


### [378] [Towards Effective Structured Context Modeling for Conversational Recommender Systems via Dual-node Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00618)
*Jincheng Zhang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DREAMS框架，通过树状结构建模对话上下文以追踪用户偏好演变，结合蒙特卡洛树搜索进行偏好探索和大语言模型优化进行偏好利用，提升推荐系统效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决对话推荐系统中多轮交互下用户偏好追踪与上下文建模的问题，旨在同时满足偏好 elicitation（获取）和 exploitation（利用）两个核心目标。

Method: 提出DREAMS框架，包含两种专用节点：1) 探索节点：利用蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)策略性探索对话动作并推断潜在用户偏好；2) 利用节点：基于大语言模型(LLM)的细化机制，将追踪到的偏好状态转化为结构化检索查询以生成推荐。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛实验证明了DREAMS框架及其设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式捕捉用户偏好演变的树状上下文建模方法能有效增强对话推荐系统的性能。

Abstract: We investigate the role of conversational context modeling in user preference tracking for Conversational Recommendation Systems (CRSs). In this regard, we propose DREAMS, a novel tree-structured context modeling framework that explicitly captures user preference evolution throughout multi-turn interactions. DREAMS introduces two specialized node types to support the two fundamental objectives of CRSs: preference elicitation and preference exploitation. Specifically, elicitation nodes leverage Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to strategically explore conversational actions and infer latent user preferences, while exploitation nodes employ LLM-based refinement to transform the tracked preference state into structured retrieval queries for recommendation. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DREAMS and its design.

</details>


### [379] [It Takes Two to Match: Co-Evolving Generative Retriever with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00638)
*Runpeng Dai,Kaili Huang,Changsung Kang,Ciya Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoGR框架，利用大语言模型直接为查询和物品生成关键词表示，通过倒排索引匹配，结合监督微调与协同进化强化学习（GRPO），在检索F1指标上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的检索方法多仅用于查询侧增强，最终匹配仍依赖下游检索器；旨在让LLM直接构建查询与物品两侧的检索表示，以兼容现有基础设施并提升匹配效果。

Method: 1. 训练LLM直接生成紧凑关键词集作为检索表示；2. 两阶段训练：先通过监督微调建立对齐的关键词空间，后采用协同进化强化学习（GRPO）交替优化查询侧和物品侧生成器；3. 优化目标均为检索F1，物品侧使用反事实边际奖励衡量其对查询侧F1的影响；4. 通过倒排索引进行关键词直接匹配。

Result: 在内部APP Marketplace数据集和公开WANDS基准上，CoGR性能优于10个代表性稀疏、密集及生成式基线，F1分别提升10.9%和36.1%；分析显示查询-物品关键词空间随训练逐渐对齐且共进化过程稳定。

Conclusion: CoGR通过端到端训练LLM生成双向关键词表示并结合协同进化强化学习，有效提升了检索性能，同时保持了与现有基于关键词的检索基础设施的兼容性。

Abstract: Retrieval is the first stage of modern search and advertising systems, selecting a candidate set from a large item universe for downstream ranking and auction. Recent work increasingly leverages LLMs to improve retrieval through query expansion, data synthesis, and retrieval-feedback training. However, the generative component is typically used for query-side augmentation, while final matching is still delegated to a downstream retriever. We introduce CoGR, a retrieval framework that instead trains LLMs to directly construct retrieval representations on both query and item sides. Each generator produces a compact set of keywords, which are matched directly through an inverted index, preserving compatibility with existing keyword-based retrieval infrastructure. CoGR uses a two-stage training pipeline. Supervised fine-tuning first establishes an aligned keyword space, after which co-evolving reinforcement learning alternately optimizes the query- and item-side generators with GRPO against the opposite side's frozen index. Both sides optimize the same query-to-item retrieval $F_1$ objective: the query side receives retrieval $F_1$ directly, while the item side receives a counterfactual marginal reward measuring the change in query-side $F_1$ caused by its generated keywords. Across 10 representative sparse, dense, and generative baselines, CoGR achieves the best performance on both an internal APP Marketplace dataset and the public WANDS benchmark, improving $F_1$ over the strongest baseline by $10.9\%$ and $36.1\%$, respectively. Further analysis shows stable co-evolution and increasingly aligned query--item keyword spaces over training.

</details>


### [380] [From Saliency to Discriminability: Rank-Preserving Visual Token Pruning for VLM Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00667)
*Siyi Liu,Hanjun Yang,Chenchen Zhang,Xiaorong Zhu,Xinyu Zuo,Lisheng Duan,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Junfu Pu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RaDiCal框架，利用归一化注意力熵解决视觉语言模型中注意力显著性与排序贡献不匹配的问题，实现高效、免训练的Token剪枝，显著提升检索效率并维持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型作为列表排序器需处理大量候选者的视觉Token，Token剪枝对部署至关重要。现有方法基于注意力显著性保留Token，但研究表明显著性与排序贡献存在系统性错位：视觉上突出的Token常捕获跨候选者的顺序无关模式，导致剪枝效果不佳。

Method: 提出RaDiCal（Rank-Discriminative Calibration），一种免训练框架。核心机制包括：1) 使用归一化注意力熵诊断注意力集中层的可靠性；2) 融合基于注意力的显著性与无注意力的秩判别先验；3) 根据信任景观选择最佳剪枝层。

Result: 在三个检索基准和多架构VLM上表现优异：在Flickr30K和MSCOCO上达到或超越Dense MRR@10性能；在FashionIQ上排名第一；在仅保留10% Token时性能下降不超过1.2个百分点。同时减少39-45%的FLOPs，并提供1.28-1.45倍的实测加速，且无需数据集特定微调。

Conclusion: RaDiCal通过结合注意力熵与秩判别先验，有效解决了显著性误导问题，证明了在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅降低计算成本是可行的，为VLM的高效部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models used as listwise rerankers must jointly process visual tokens from tens of candidates per query, making token pruning essential for practical deployment. Existing pruning methods retain tokens by attention saliency, yet we show that saliency is systematically misaligned with ranking contribution: visually prominent tokens often capture order-neutral patterns shared across candidates. This mismatch is layer-dependent: saliency becomes informative only where attention is concentrated, and normalized attention entropy diagnoses the reliability shift (Pearson r=0.87). We propose RaDiCal (Rank-Discriminative Calibration), a training-free framework that uses normalized attention entropy to decide when saliency can be trusted, fusing it with an attention-free rank-discriminative prior and selecting pruning layers from the same trust landscape. Across three retrieval benchmarks and multiple VLM architectures, RaDiCal matches Dense MRR@10 on Flickr30K and surpasses it on MSCOCO at a 20% token budget, ranks first among all pruning methods on FashionIQ, and holds within 1.2 pp on Flickr30K and MSCOCO at 10% retention. It cuts FLOPs by 39--45% and delivers 1.28--1.45$\times$ measured speedups across two VLM architectures without dataset-specific retuning.

</details>


### [381] [SwapRec: Warming Up Cold Items Through Training-Time Swaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00913)
*Marta Moscati,Jan Malte Lichtenberg,Davide Abbattista,Antonio De Candia,Laura Boggia,Matteo Ruffini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对冷启动物品交互导致推荐系统实时个性化失效的问题，提出SwapRec方法。通过在训练阶段模拟推理时的“冷热替换”策略，显著提升了包含冷启动物品的推荐准确率及冷启动物品的曝光率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于ID的推荐系统中，与冷启动物品（Cold Items）的交互会破坏用户偏好估计，而排除这些物品又阻碍了实时推荐更新。工业界常用启发式方法在推理时将冷启动物品替换为相似的“热”物品（Warm Items），但现有研究表明序列模型对此类替换并不鲁棒。

Method: 提出了SwapRec方法，其核心思想是在训练阶段就应用与推理阶段相同的“替换”启发式策略（即使用物品的侧信息将冷启动物品替换为最相似的热物品）。该方法被应用于最先进的序列推荐模型中，并在在线购物、电影和音乐三个领域进行了定量实验分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论底层序列架构如何，SwapRec都能显著提高存在冷启动物品交互时的推荐准确性，同时增加了推荐列表中冷启动物品的比例。

Conclusion: SwapRec是一种易于实现的方案，通过训练时模拟推理时的数据分布偏差，有效解决了冷启动物品对实时个性化推荐的负面影响，提升了模型的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Interactions with cold items negatively impact real-time personalization of ID-based recommender systems. This is because the use of such interactions degrades user preference estimates, whereas excluding cold items from the user profile prevents real-time recommendation updates. In industrial scenarios, one heuristic often applied to address this shortcoming at inference time is to replace, i.e., "swap", cold-start items by their most similar "warm" neighbor, where similarity is inferred from the items' side information. In this paper, we demonstrate that sequential models, most often used for real-time personalization, are not robust to such swaps, and propose SwapRec, an approach to address this issue. SwapRec relies on using the same swap heuristics already at training time. We apply SwapRec to state-of-the-art models for sequential recommendation and analyze its impact by means of quantitative experiments in three recommendation domains (online shopping, movie, music). The experimental results show that, irrespective of the underlying sequential architecture, our easy-to-implement SwapRec approach allows for substantially more accurate recommendations when in presence of interactions with cold items, simultaneously leading to a larger percentage of cold items in the recommendation lists.

</details>


### [382] [TGR: Advancing Industrial Recommendation from Generative-Paradigm Ranking toward Unified Generation and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00986)
*TGR Team,Lei Cheng,Haonan Hu,Beibei Kong,Yudong Li,Zang Li,Yunsheng Pang,Hongyang Su,Jianchao Tu,Yunlong Wang,Bing Wen,Junzhang Zhu,Shaojie Zhu,Chengxiang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 腾讯提出TGR（腾讯生成式推荐）工业框架，旨在突破传统级联推荐系统的局限。该框架通过生成式范式，从排序、端到端生成和推理三个方向进行革新，并在腾讯大规模生产环境中部署，显著提升了CTR、广告收入及新用户冷启动效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的工业推荐系统依赖级联的检索、预排序、排序和重排序阶段。这种架构存在模型单独优化导致的扩展性受限、决策碎片化以及缺乏语义知识和推理能力的问题。

Method: 提出TGR框架，包含三个核心组件：1. TGR-GenRank：利用CCFormer升级排序模块，结合统一特征标记化、可扩展Transformer主干、特征-域分离交叉注意力等机制；2. TGR-GenRec：探索端到端生成，包括BARGE（解决层级语义-ID生成的边界损失和语义漂移）和HiGR（执行全列表生成，采用前缀结构化语义ID和粗到细解码）；3. TGR-Reason：将离线生成的语义-ID推理标记注入在线解码过程，实现无需请求时展开的推理。

Result: TGR已在腾讯服务数亿用户的生产线上部署。具体成效包括：CCFormer在5个A/B测试场景中显著提升性能，其中两个完全上线后带来+3.57% CTR和+1.71%广告收入增长；BARGE使Hit@5提升10.2-16.9%，并带来+0.60% CTR和+1.70%阅读时长增加；HiGR使离线列表质量提升15.9-21.3%，推理速度提升5倍，并实现最高+1.22%观看时长和+1.73%视频播放量增长；TGR-Reason使冷启动新用户的Hit@1提升477.8%，有效消费提升+1.75%，新用户曝光转化率提升+13.09%。

Conclusion: TGR框架成功将推荐系统推向生成式范式，通过整合先进的Transformer架构、端到端生成策略和推理机制，在保持高效推理的同时，大幅提升了推荐系统的准确性、业务指标表现及对新用户的适应能力，证明了生成式推荐在超大规模工业场景中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems typically rely on cascaded retrieval, pre-ranking, ranking, and reranking stages, whose separately optimized models limit scaling, fragment decision making, and lack semantic knowledge and reasoning. We present TGR (Tencent Generative Recommendation), an industrial framework that advances recommendation toward the generative paradigm along three coupled directions. TGR-GenRank upgrades ranking through CCFormer, which combines unified feature tokenization, a scalable Transformer backbone, feature-field separated cross attention, subspace token mixing, and hierarchical sequence compression while retaining per-item multi-task outputs. TGR-GenRec explores end-to-end generation under two paradigms: BARGE bridges item-boundary loss and semantic drift in hierarchical semantic-ID generation through item context-aware attention, hierarchical path reranking, and orthogonal dual-path decoding; HiGR performs whole-slate generation with prefix-structured semantic IDs, coarse-to-fine decoding, and listwise multi-objective alignment. TGR-Reason injects offline-generated semantic-ID reason tokens into online decoding, providing reasoning without request-time rollout. TGR is deployed across Tencent production surfaces serving hundreds of millions of users. CCFormer delivers significant gains in five A/B-tested scenarios and is fully launched in two, including +3.57% CTR and +1.71% advertising revenue. BARGE improves Hit@5 by 10.2-16.9% and yields +0.60% CTR and +1.70% reading time after full rollout. HiGR improves offline slate quality by 15.9-21.3% with a 5x inference speedup and achieves up to +1.22% watch time and +1.73% video views. TGR-Reason raises cold-start new-user Hit@1 by 477.8% and delivers +1.75% effective consumption and +13.09% new-user exposure-to-conversion online.

</details>


### [383] [Web Price Extraction: State of the Art and an Adaptive Browserless Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01030)
*Evgeniia Kositsyna,Jorge Lloret-Gazo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应无浏览器价格提取系统，结合HTML分块、语法规则及贝叶斯/遗传算法优化，显著提升精度至87.3%并降低处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页价格提取方法在准确性与可扩展性之间存在权衡：传统手动包装器维护成本高且适应性差；基于浏览器的方法资源消耗大；纯规则方法缺乏鲁棒性；机器学习/LLM方法需要大量数据和算力。旨在解决无浏览器方案在应对网站结构差异时的鲁棒性问题，同时保持低计算成本。

Method: 构建基线架构，结合HTML页面分块与句法、语义及频率规则；引入两种扩展机制：1) 贝叶斯方法动态更新规则权重；2) 遗传算法优化系统全局参数。形成混合自适应框架。

Result: 相比基线模型，新系统的精确率从77.2%提升至87.3%，平均每页处理时间减少约14%。证明了该方法在保持低计算成本的同时，能提供具有竞争力的提取精度。

Conclusion: 该混合自适应无浏览器价格提取系统是一种高效、高精度且低资源消耗的替代方案，优于手动调优的无浏览器方案以及高资源的浏览器或LLM方法。

Abstract: Price extraction from websites is a key task for market monitoring, price comparison, and business analytics in e-commerce. Existing approaches can be broadly divided into four groups, and understanding their trade-offs in accuracy and scalability is essential for selecting suitable extraction strategies. Classical methods rely on manually written wrappers and rule induction from labeled pages, offering high accuracy but adapting poorly to structural changes and requiring considerable maintenance effort. Browser-based methods, using tools such as Selenium and Puppeteer, handle dynamic JavaScript content but consume large computational resources and scale poorly. Browserless approaches retrieve HTML directly via HTTP requests, offering significant gains in speed and cost, but rely on rules calibrated for specific sites. Methods based on machine learning and large language models offer adaptability but require training data and substantial computation.
  Our main contribution is an adaptive browserless price extraction system that improves robustness to structural differences between websites. We implemented a baseline architecture combining HTML page fragmentation with syntactic, semantic, and frequency rules, and extended it in two ways: a Bayesian approach that dynamically updates rule weights, and a genetic algorithm that optimizes the system's global parameters. This hybrid scheme increased precision from 77.2% to 87.3% and reduced average per-page processing time by approximately 14% relative to the baseline, confirming it as a competitive alternative to manually tuned browserless solutions and to more resource-intensive browser- or LLM-based methods, offering high extraction accuracy at low computational cost.

</details>


### [384] [World Model-Guided Reinforcement Learning via Counterfactual User Engagement Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01067)
*Ang Li,Xin Xu,Bin Liang,Yue Ma,Fubang Zhao,Yangyang Kang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出WMG-RL框架，利用冻结的用户参与度世界模型（UEWM）进行反事实模拟，为推荐策略提供密集奖励信号，使小型学生模型在推荐任务上达到或超越大型LLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于在线反馈的强化学习在用户中心智能体中面临的成本高、延迟大、风险高以及缺乏同一用户状态下的反事实比较等问题。

Method: 构建用户参与度世界模型（UEWM），将推荐项目视为智能体动作，用户异构反馈视为环境观测。UEWM从参与历史中学习特定用户的动态，并在WMG-RL框架中并行预测候选项目的反馈，将其转化为密集奖励以优化下游策略。

Result: UEWM提供了跨领域可靠且可迁移的奖励信号；WMG-RL使得一个紧凑的1.7B参数学生策略在下游推荐任务上能够匹配或超越更大规模的大语言模型（LLMs）。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于世界模型的反事实用户参与模拟，WMG-RL有效克服了传统强化学习的局限，实现了高效且高性能的个性化推荐。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for user-centric agents is limited by the cost, latency, and risk of collecting online feedback, as well as by the lack of counterfactual comparisons under the same user state. In this paper, we propose World Model-Guided Reinforcement Learning via counterfactual user engagement simulation (WMG-RL), a framework in which a frozen user simulator provides reward supervision before real user exposure. Motivated by language world models, we instantiate the simulator as a User Engagement World Model (UEWM), which treats a recommended item as the agent action and the user's heterogeneous feedback as the environment observation. Rather than learning one fixed environment transition, UEWM learns to infer user-specific dynamics from engagement history and apply them to candidate items. In WMG-RL, a downstream policy proposes multiple candidate items for the same history; UEWM predicts the corresponding engagement feedback in parallel; and the simulated feedback is converted into dense rewards for policy optimization. Experiments show that UEWM provides reliable and transferable reward signals across domains, and that WMG-RL enables a compact 1.7B student policy to match or surpass much larger LLMs on downstream recommendation tasks.

</details>


### [385] [From Language to Behavior: Scaling Sequence Transformers for Industrial Recommendation Ranking with Rec-Native Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01240)
*Jie Chen,Xiangqian Yu,Yanchao Lian,Tan Lu,Run Yang,Zhengchun Shang,Xing Wang,Cheng Chen,Ke Hu,Qiang Li,Tianjiu Yin,Xiaobing Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ReST框架，通过序列编码器优化信号质量和计算解码器解决计算不对称性，在推荐排序中实现高效扩展，在线A/B测试显著提升AUC和收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Transformer扩展到行为序列建模面临挑战：推荐场景中信号质量低（噪声大、时间不规则、稀疏监督）且存在计算不对称性（单次请求需对大量候选者评分，受限于严格延迟预算）。

Method: 提出ReST框架：1. 针对信号质量，引入双门控注意力、旋转位置与时间嵌入、稳定残差归一化及训练辅助目标；2. 针对计算不对称性，分解为重型可重用编码器和轻量级交叉解码器（无投影KV注意力），结合共享前缀训练与服务以实现“一次计算，多次解码”。

Result: 在工业和公共基准上，ReST在准确性上更高，并沿序列长度、深度和宽度更一致地扩展；在线A/B测试显示P99延迟50ms内，AUC提升1.31%，核心收入指标提升11.93%。

Conclusion: ReST已成功全量部署于生产环境，证明行为序列扩展是生产排序中一个有前景且未被充分挖掘的方向。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers has driven large gains in language modeling, but transplanting this to behavior-sequence modeling in production ranking is challenging: recommendation differs in signal quality, where behavior sequences are noisy, temporally irregular, and sparsely supervised, and in computation asymmetry, where each request scores many candidates against one shared user history under tight latency budgets. We propose ReST, a recommendation-native Transformer scaling framework. For signal quality, it introduces a sequence encoder with dual-gated attention, rotary positional and temporal embedding, stabilized residual normalization, and training-only auxiliary objectives. For computation asymmetry, it factorizes ranking into a heavy reusable encoder and a lightweight cross decoder with projection-free KV attention and token-specific parameterization, coupling user-level shared-prefix training with shared-prefix serving for compute-once, decode-many-times ranking. Across industrial and public benchmarks, ReST achieves higher accuracy and scales more consistently along sequence length, depth, and width, where LLM-style Transformer blocks saturate. A one-week online A/B test on a production advertising platform improves online AUC by 1.31% and lifts a core revenue metric by 11.93% within a 50 ms P99 budget; ReST has since been fully deployed in production, showing that behavior-sequence scaling remains a promising, under-exploited axis for production ranking.

</details>


### [386] [MIDR: Enrichment-Augmented Indexing for Multimodal Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01316)
*Debanjan Mahata,Atharva Tendle,Daniel Preotiuc-Pietro,Yong Zhuang,Ozan Irsoy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MIDR框架，通过索引时多模态推理替代检索时的视觉晚期交互，以更低内存和延迟实现高精度文档检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决富视觉文档检索中的表示问题：传统OCR会线性化、损坏或遗漏表格、图表及布局关系中的重要内容；现有ColPali等视觉检索器虽保留图像信息，但增加查询服务路径负担。

Method: 引入MIDR（Multimodal Indexing for Document Retrieval），一种无需训练的增强型索引框架。在数据摄入阶段，利用多模态大语言模型将渲染页面转化为经验证的文本字段，结合BM25F进行索引，并可融合稠密检索；从而在服务端采用以文本为中心的检索策略，同时保持对多模态证据 grounding。

Result: 在ViDoRe V3基准测试中，MIDR Hybrid在五个英文领域平均nDCG达0.6219，较BM25提升23.0%，并与ColQwen2.5相当；在两个法语领域，通过英语查询与法语页面文本的对齐，nDCG从0.1532升至0.5448，超越ColQwen2.5。总体在七个领域中四项优于ColQwen2.5，且索引内存减少约9倍，查询延迟降低约2倍。

Conclusion: 索引时间的多模态推理是替代服务时间视觉晚期交互的一种极具吸引力的方案，能在保证准确率的同时显著优化部署效率（内存与延迟）。

Abstract: Retrieval over visually rich documents has a representation problem: important content often lives in tables, charts, figures, and layout relations that plain OCR linearizes, corrupts, or omits. ColPali-family visual retrievers address this with patch-level multi-vector indexes and late-interaction scoring, keeping image-derived retrieval on the query-time serving path. We introduce MIDR (Multimodal Indexing for Document Retrieval), a training-free framework for enrichment-augmented indexing that shifts multimodal reasoning to index time. During ingestion, a multimodal LLM converts rendered pages into verified textual fields that are indexed with BM25F and optionally fused with dense retrieval, enabling text-centric serving over multimodally grounded evidence. On ViDoRe V3, MIDR Hybrid achieves 0.6219 average nDCG across five English domains, a 23.0% relative gain over BM25, remaining competitive with ColQwen2.5. On two French-document domains, enrichment bridges English queries and French page text, lifting BM25 from 0.1532 to 0.5448 nDCG and outperforming ColQwen2.5. Across all seven domains, MIDR leads ColQwen2.5 on four while using approximately 9x smaller index memory and approximately 2x lower query latency. These results establish index-time multimodal reasoning as a compelling accuracy-deployment alternative to serving-time visual late interaction.

</details>


### [387] [AutoConcept: Training-Free Concept-Guided Reranking for Metadata-Available Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01456)
*Tianyu Wang,Tianjiao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AutoConcept的免训练重排序器，利用元数据将概念证据转化为可解释的记忆，通过过滤噪声、激活相关约束及推理时校准，显著提升了组合图像检索（CIR）在FashionIQ和LinCIR数据集上的早期排名性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对组合图像检索（CIR）中，固定模型返回候选池后缺乏有效第二阶重排序机制的问题，旨在利用可用的画廊元数据进行概念引导的评分，以提升检索精度。

Method: 引入AutoConcept，一种无需训练的重新排序器。该方法将概念证据转换为可解释记忆，具体包括：过滤噪声概念、通过辅助负惩罚激活与查询相关的正约束、以及在推理时结合基础检索分数与基于元数据的概念-候选对齐进行校准。

Result: 在FashionIQ数据集上相比WeiMoCIR取得显著的早期排名提升，并在LinCIR候选池上保持一致的插件式增益。实验表明，结构化概念记忆提供了超越直接查询文本和提取属性匹配的额外信号；人类标注研究证实该记忆接口同样适用于处理人工提供的证据。

Conclusion: AutoConcept被定位为一种适用于拥有可用元数据的产品风格组合图像检索库的可解释概念记忆重排序器，证明了概念级重排序的有效性。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) retrieves a target image from a reference image and a text modification. This paper studies metadata-available CIR reranking, where a fixed CIR model first returns a candidate pool and gallery metadata is then used for second-stage concept-guided scoring. We introduce AutoConcept, a training-free reranker that converts concept evidence into an interpretable memory. AutoConcept filters noisy concepts, activates query-relevant positive constraints with an auxiliary negative penalty, and combines base retrieval scores with metadata-based concept-candidate alignment through inference-time calibration. On FashionIQ, AutoConcept yields significant early-rank improvements over WeiMoCIR and consistent plug-in gains on LinCIR candidate pools. Metadata-aware controls show that structured concept memory adds signal beyond direct query-text and extracted-attribute matching, while a query-only variant further supports the effectiveness of concept-level reranking. A supplementary real-human concept-label study indicates that the same memory interface can consume participant-provided evidence. These results position AutoConcept as an interpretable concept-memory reranker for product-style CIR galleries with available metadata.

</details>
